  `mes-typingtest:v1`. **Device label (both typing pages):** a posted score carries `d` = `k` keyboard / `p` phone / `t` tablet (`myDevice()`: iPad / iPadOS-as-Mac / Android without "Mobile" with touch = tablet; other touch = phone; the rest = keyboard); the API stores it
  in the score's fraction (+0.125 / +0.25 / +0.375; none = posted before it existed), rows return `d`, `GET ?dev=k|p|t` filters server-side (ranks renumbered), the table shows a ⌨ / 📱 / tablet icon and has an All / Keyboard / Phone / Tablet filter. Self-reported.
  **Voice label** (2026-10-06): a score can also carry `d` = `v` (🎤, stored as +0.4375 in the fraction): the Typing Test flags a run as voice typing when the input box gets an `insertFromDictation` event or two or more events that add several words at once (a keyboard, even a swipe one, commits one word at a time); the post box says it will be marked, the board has a Voice chip + `?dev=v`. Self-reported and heuristic, like the other labels; old scores can only be relabelled with a one-off Redis script. Scores posted before the label existed were backfilled on 2026-10-05 (all Keyboard except two known phone scores) with a one-off script run against Redis, not kept in the repo.
  `mes-typingtest:v1`. **Searchable voice list:** `main_js/voice-picker.js` (`MESVoicePicker.enhance(select)`) turns a native voice `<select>` into a button + panel with a search box (name, language, country, online), a preview
  button per voice and keyboard support, mirroring the select (so the page's own code is unchanged); used here and on `/speedreader` (`pre_js` + one `enhance` call). Registered in `add_tool_page_controls` (floating bar
  returns to `/typing-test`), `add_cross_links`, `build_search_index`, `organize_hub_cards`; deliberately NOT given the bottom ad (the Typing Test has none either, though `add_bottom_ad.py` lists both: strip
  `MES-BOTTOM-AD` after running it). Logos are the Typing Test's (`img/typing-test-transcribe-logo.png` is a copy). After editing run `build_tool_apps.py --only=typing-test-transcribe,typing-test --apply` then
  `add_tool_page_controls.py --apply` (`git checkout` the churn on other tool pages).
- **Solar System Today** (2026-10-03, `mes.fm/solar-system-today`, indigo `#3730a3`; `/earth-today`, `/solar-system`, `/orrery`, `/planets` 308 to it; card in `tools.html` "Sky & Space"). Where the Sun,
  planets, Pluto, the Moon and Halley's Comet are on any date (clamped 1000-3000), with playback from real time to 100 years/s. Built by `build_tool_apps.py` from `tool_apps_src/solar-system-today/`;
  the page script is `lib.js` + `view.js` + `app.js` (new `js_parts` option) and `extra_js` also writes `mes.fm/solar-system-today/js/orrery-embed.js` = `lib.js` + `view.js` + `embed.js`, the limited
  widget on `mes.fm/moon` ("Earth, Moon & the Solar System Right Now": Earth & Moon / inner / whole system, play, +-1 day, Now; Ctrl+scroll zooms so the page still scrolls). **All astronomy is Astronomy
  Engine** (the copy `mes.fm/moon/js/astronomy.browser.min.js` already ships; the page loads it via `pre_js`, so don't move that file): `lib.js` = positions (heliocentric ecliptic J2000),
  geocentric view (constellation, elongation, magnitude, retrograde), Moon info, `events()` (next moon quarters, lunar/solar eclipse, equinoxes/solstices, Earth perihelion/aphelion, oppositions, greatest
  elongations -- each a "Jump" row), Halley (two-body from 1986 elements, good to a few months), zodiac boundaries. Node tests: `node tool_apps_src/solar-system-tests.js`. `view.js` = canvas renderer: orrery
  (drag = rotate/tilt, shift/right-drag pan, wheel/pinch zoom, "Squeeze distances" = r^p with p = 1-0.62*c so every planet fits, trails computed live, belts / Trojans as Kepler-moving particles, Saturn
  rings, procedural Earth/Moon/Jupiter), Earth & Moon (Sun fixed on the left so phases read in order; no shadow cone on purpose -- the 5 degree orbit tilt would imply an eclipse every full moon; "True
  scale" toggle; phase inset with maria) and the zodiac band. Wide page like the graphing calculators (not in `add_sidebar.py`), Theatre via `pageMode`. Dark-theme gotcha: the planetarium stage colours are
  custom properties (`--ss-bg`...) because `add_tool_page_controls.py` would lighten a literal near-black background. Logos are PIL placeholders (`solar-system-today/img/logo.png`, `logo-big.png`,
  `img/solar-system-today-logo.png`) until Grok art lands. `build_tool_apps.py --only=slug,slug` now rebuilds just those apps.

- **Search Engines** (2026-10-03, `mes.fm/search-engines`, burnt orange `#c2410c`; `/searchengines`, `/engines`, `/multisearch`, `/search-all` 308 to it; card in `tools.html` "Media & Web").
  One search typed once, opened in many engines. Built by `build_tool_apps.py` from `tool_apps_src/search-engines/` (`lib.js` = the 71-engine table + URL builder, node tests in
  `tool_apps_src/search-engines-tests.js`; `app.js` = UI). Templates use `{q}` and `{v}` (a variant: Google domains .com/.ca/.co.uk..., Bing market, DuckDuckGo region, Yahoo host, Yandex,
  Amazon, Wikipedia languages; every ticked variant is its own target). Search type (web / images / news / videos) and time range append per-engine params; engines that can't do a type
  are skipped and listed. `site:` / exact phrase / `-site:` are added only to engines flagged `ops`. Open modes: new tabs, **tiled windows** (`window.open` popup features computed by
  `tile()` from `screen.avail*`), same tab (individual links become normal anchors, so middle-click works). `window.open` is called synchronously in the click and its null return is the
  popup-blocker detector (browsers allow ONE popup per click until the site is allowed pop-ups: blocked targets go into a queue shown with a big "Open next" button, one click per engine, plus allow-pop-ups instructions; the "One at a time" mode does that on purpose) (so `noopener` is NOT passed; `w.opener = null` instead): blocked links are listed. Sets (main, privacy, censorship & bias, video censorship, SEO, AI,
  social, research, everything) + the user's own named sets (shift-click deletes), own engines (`%s` / `{q}`, http(s) only), recent searches, JSON backup/restore, `?q=&e=&t=&w=&m=` share
  links (`&a=google:images+news` = the per-card extras; since 2026-10-07 also `&v=google:ca+co.uk,bing:gb` = ticked variants and `&ex=site.com` = leave-out site, so a link carries the whole setup except your own engines). Two buttons copy it: "Copy share link" in the tools row and "🔗 Share this search" next to Open; a page opened from a shared link toasts "Shared search loaded: press Open" (the browser needs a click before it will open tabs). Each engine card with image/news/video searches has an "Also: Images / News / Videos" row of chips (`S.extra`, `targets(..., extra)`): ticking one opens that search IN ADDITION to the page-wide type (so Google web + Google Images together; every ticked variant gets each type). localStorage `mes-search-engines:v1`. Engine URL formats drift: re-check a few now and then (the table is the only place to fix). **Since 2026-10-11 a normal-width page in `add_sidebar.py`'s tools collection (Standard | Wide | Theatre + "More like this", Wide by default via the inline `pageMode` snippet) and a browser default-search candidate:** `mes.fm/search-engines/opensearch.xml` (+ `head_extra` `<link rel="search">`, new `build_tool_apps.py` option) and a "Set as my default search…" card at the top (`#se-def`, per-browser steps for Brave / Chrome / Edge / Firefox / Safari; the URL to add is `https://mes.fm/search-engines?q=%s`). A `?q=` visit with no `&e=` focuses Open so Enter launches the ticked engines (browsers only allow tabs after a gesture). `main_js/track.js` drops all query params on `/search-engines` so people's searches never reach the stats.

- `set_posts_tile.py` -- sets the **Posts tile** image of `mes.fm/911` and `mes.fm/bg` (`img/911-posts-icon.jpg`, `img/bg-posts-icon.jpg`, 900x600) to a new post. **Standing rule (2026-10-06): whenever you mirror a post into those Posts lists, run it with `--slug <new-mirror-slug> --apply` so the tiles use that page's FIRST image (centre-cropped), unless the user names another image / crop** (`--index N`, `--image URL|file`, `--anchor top|center|bottom`, `--box x0,y0,x1,y1` = red highlight box in source pixels, crop centres on it, e.g. a "has blocked you" screenshot). Also bumps `iconVersion` of the posts page in each hub's `build.mjs` and runs `npm run build` there (`--hubs 911,bg` to limit). Dry-runs by default; `--apply` writes. The mirror's own og:image / list-card thumbnail is separate (set in the page + `sections.mjs`).
- `fix_image_case_references.py` -- rewrites image references (src / data-src / srcset / og:image / twitter:image, root-relative, relative or `https://mes.fm/...`) whose filename differs from the file on disk only by letter case (`eyebrows.jpg` vs `eyebrows.JPG`): macOS ignores case so it looked fine locally, Vercel (Linux) 404ed (2026-10-05: 255 refs in 54 meme / dream-home / quote pages; found via Site Index "og:image file missing" + "broken local reference"). Only the filename part changes; files are not renamed, so external links to the current URLs keep working. Uses directory listings, not `os.path.exists()` (case-insensitive on macOS). Idempotent; **dry-runs by default (`-v` lists), `--apply` writes.**
- `make_hires_logos.py` -- crisp 512px tile thumbnails for the legacy 2013-2018 calculator sites (percentage, grade, gpa, bmi, inflation, pokemongo, timer): their square `img/logo.png` is only 176px, so the homepage Popular tiles, search results and sidebar cards (all drawn at 180-370 CSS px) showed it 2-4x enlarged and blurry beside the 512px logos of the newer tools. Upscales `img/logo-big.png` (250px, the only larger copy of the art) to `<site>/img/logo-512.png` (Lanczos x2, unsharp mask, Lanczos down; alpha dropped so the tile is a full colour square). `logo.png` / `logo-big.png` are untouched (headers, favicons, og:image). `build_search_index.py` prefers `logo-512.png` over `logo.png` when present; after running this re-run `build_search_index.py` then `build_aside_recs.py --apply`. `sjwkeyboard` is skipped (photographic, ~200 KB). Replace a `logo-512.png` with real hi-res art any time (same name; `--force` re-makes it). Idempotent; **dry-runs by default, `--apply` writes.**
- `fix_chapter_headings.py` -- the top-level chapter folds of the long collapsible articles (Problems Plus, cubic formula, ferrocell, Tom Sky, Norman-Patricia, Nancy physics report: all clones of the vector-functions-problems-plus shell) were `<h1 class="chapter-toggle-header">`, so a page had 4-20 `<h1>`s beside its title (Site Index "More than one h1"). Now `<h2 class="chapter-toggle-header chapter-top">`, with the shell's `.post-body h1` declarations copied to `.post-body h2.chapter-top` so the look is unchanged (verified: identical computed size / alignment / margins); the extra `chapter-top` class is needed because the *sub*-section folds are already plain `h2.chapter-toggle-header` with their own look. Patches the six pages, the five `build.mjs` generators, `nancy_physics_src/content.html` and `pdf_to_content.py` (top level emits h2 + chapter-top), so rebuilds keep it; `build_nancy_physics_article.py` inherits the CSS from the vector-functions shell. New pages cloned from those shells: run it. `911-wtc-vehicle-massacre`'s stray second h1 ("Screenshots of Video") was fixed by hand. Idempotent; **dry-runs by default, `--apply` writes.**
- `check_external_links.py` -- **read-only** dead-link report (2026-10-05: 2,577 distinct external links on 1,270 pages, ~3 min). Judges per platform: YouTube watch / shorts / live / playlist / youtu.be via oEmbed (a plain GET of a deleted video still says 200; 404 = dead, 401 = private / embedding off = "restricted"), everything else HEAD then GET (404 / 410 / DNS / refused / TLS = dead), bot walls (x.com, facebook, instagram, linkedin, pinterest, tiktok) and 401 / 403 / 429 = "blocked" (never called dead), 5xx / timeouts retried then "flaky"; mes.fm's own links are checked on disk first. Per-host concurrency + delay, so it is polite. `--out report.json`, `--host`, `--limit`. **Re-test every DEAD hit with `curl -L` before acting**: first run had false positives (Python's cert store on news.gov.bc.ca, Google help / Wolfram answering a script 404, OneDrive share links that redirect to a sign-in page and can't be verified). Run it now and then; don't schedule it.
- `fix_pagination_dead_ends.py` -- Previous / Next Page buttons (and `<link rel=next|prev>`) pointing at pages that don't exist: the paginated bmicalculator/sports "Top 10 ... Player BMI" articles' last page offered "Next Page" -> `/3` or `/4`, and page 2's "Previous Page" -> `<article>/1` (page 1 is the article page itself). `/1` Previous becomes the parent page when it exists, any other dead button is removed (5 pages, 10 links). Cleanurls aware; idempotent; **dry-runs by default (`-v`), `--apply` writes.**
- `fix_mobile_apps_footer_link.py` -- the shared footer's "Mobile Apps" link pointed at `https://mes.fm/mobile-apps.html` on ~900 pages (classic calculator / meme / quote pages + `tool_page_template.html`); it now points at `https://mes.fm/mobile-apps`. Vercel's cleanUrls 308ed the old form, so nothing was broken, but it cost a redirect hop per click and let Google discover the `.html` address (the only Search Console "Soft 404", 2026-10-16). Only the href changes; skips `_http*`, `node_modules`, `percentagecalculator-app/`. Idempotent; **dry-runs by default (-v lists), `--apply` writes.** Pages cloned from an old template: re-run it.
- `fix_relative_canonicals.py` -- rewrites root-relative `<link rel="canonical" href="/path">` to `https://mes.fm/path` (211 pages on 2026-10-03: all puzzles, the memes/ gallery items, the card hubs, contact / donate / privacy-policy, the homepage; found by the Site Index "Canonical is not absolute" issue). Only the href changes. HTTrack `_http*` folders skipped; idempotent; **dry-runs by default (-v lists pages), `--apply` writes.** New classic pages should carry an absolute canonical from the start.

- **PizzaGate hub** (2026-10-08, `mes.fm/pg` + `mes.fm/pg-series`; `pg/build.mjs` cloned from `bg/build.mjs`, content in `pg/sections.mjs`, newest part first -- Parts 1-32 converted from the Hive post `peakd.com/pg/@mes/videos`, whose top section is the hub's Important Links). Tiles: Video Series (`img/pg-series-icon.jpg` = Part 32's thumbnail, refresh when a part is added) and MES Livestreams (-> `/livestreams#pizzagate`, new "PizzaGate" chip in `livestreams/build.mjs` `CATEGORIES`). Also a tile on `/conspiracy` (`img/pg-icon.jpg`) and a first entry in the PizzaGate list of `links/index.html`. **No AdSense on any PizzaGate page** (the generator emits no loader; `hub-theatre.js` only gives the sidebar an ad slot when the page itself loads AdSense). Logos `img/pg-logo.jpg` / `pg-logo-big.jpg` are PIL placeholders until real art lands.
- `add_hub_filter.py` -- **search box + topic chips on the long hub list pages** (2026-10-13; same widget as `/livestreams`, `/math-qa`, `/experiments-series`, now in one shared module `mes.fm/shared/hub-filter.mjs` = `FILTER_CSS`, `FILTER_JS`, `buildFilter`, `withCats`; the older inline copies in `math/`, `experiments/`, `pg/`, `livestreams/` are untouched). Patches the `build.mjs` of `911`, `hutchison`, `conspiracy`, `science` (import + a marked `MES-HUB-FILTER` block generated from the script's `HUB_FILTERS` table + data-cats on cards/rows + filter bar + CSS/JS) and is idempotent (`--apply`; then `npm run build` in each hub and `git checkout` its `link-meta.json`). Pages: `911-spooks` (chips = the people: Bob Greenyer, 9/11 Revisionist, Abby Martin, David Hughes, Ryan Bannister, Andrew Mason, Off Guardian), `911-posts`, `911-videos`, `911truth`, `911-observable-evidence` (search only), `highlights`, `hutchison-interviews`, `conspiracy-posts`, `science-videos`. **To add a person / topic:** add a `(id, label, regex)` chip to `HUB_FILTERS`, re-run, rebuild. A chip matches the item's title (+ optional `kw` words); an item can instead carry `who: "bob ryan"` in `sections.mjs` (wins over the regexes; used for 911-revisionist-slander-mes and 911-spammer-dust-baggie, whose titles do not name everyone they are about). Items that match no chip fall under "Everything else". Search-only (`chips: []`) was added the same day to the 7-10 item sections: `911-short-videos`, `1109-keo-meteor-music`, `physics`, `blockchain-tutorials` (crypto-tutorials), `bg-videos`, `mh370-videos`, `antigravity-series`. Sections with 3-6 items (hutchison posts / news / unedited footage / articles, science posts / tutorials, conspiracy videos, bg / mh370 posts, free-energy) have no filter on purpose; add one to `HUB_FILTERS` (and the hub to the script's `HUB_FILTERS` keys) if they grow.

The Peakd link for this video was replaced by the mes.fm page in `math/youtube-hive-links.json` (so `/youtube` links there; the List View label still says "Hive") and in the Tom Sky timestamp list.
- **MES Experiments hub** (2026-10-08, `mes.fm/experiments` + `mes.fm/experiments-series`; `experiments/build.mjs` cloned from `bg/build.mjs` **with AdSense kept**, content in `experiments/sections.mjs`, newest first -- #55 down to the introduction (0), converted from the `#MESExperiments` entries of `peakd.com/mesexperiments/@mes/list`; its Anti-Gravity / MES Physics sections are not included yet). The OneDrive short URL `/antigravity` is now `/antigravity-onedrive`. Hub tiles: Anti-Gravity Series (`img/antigravity-series-icon.jpg` = newest video's thumbnail), MES Experiments, MES Science; the hub itself has tiles on `/science` and `/experiments` (`img/antigravity-icon.jpg`), and `/experiments` got a MES Science tile. **Logo / share art (`img/antigravity-logo.jpg` 512, `antigravity-logo-big.jpg` 1200x630, `antigravity-icon.jpg` 900x600) are crops of Part 6's thumbnail until the real art lands** (same file names).

Art = the "Nuclear Physics.jpeg" from the Part 2 video folder (`img/free-energy-logo.jpg` / `-logo-big.jpg` / `-icon.jpg`). **The sibling hubs reference each other through tile-only entries** (`science`, `experiments`, `antigravity`, `free-energy`, `hutchison`, plus `cold-fusion-lenr` on science / free-energy and `science-videos` on free-energy / hutchison / antigravity / experiments); each hub's `PAGES` array holds them, icon = `img/<slug>-icon.jpg`. A new sibling hub: add its tile to each of those `build.mjs` files (the tile helper was `tile()` in the one-off generator) and rebuild; `git checkout science/link-meta.json` after a science build.

To restore a cross tile, add a tile-only entry (`slug`, `tileLabel`, `iconVersion`, `tileOnly: true`; icon = `img/<slug>-icon.jpg`) to that hub's `PAGES`.
- **MH370 Posts** (2026-10-08): `mes.fm/mh370-posts` (new section `mh370-posts` in `mh370/sections.mjs`, Posts tab in the info bar, tile `img/mh370-posts-icon.jpg` = top of the newest post's first screenshot) lists the mirror `mes.fm/mh370-drew-ponder-spammer` (copied from the Bob Greenyer subscriber mirror's shell; its Hive title has a character lost between "Drew Ponder" and "Ashton Forbes", so the page title reads without it). The same post is first in `conspiracy/sections.mjs` posts. The BMI "What is BMI?" page puts its two transparent chart images on a white card in dark mode (rule at the end of the page's last `<style>`).
- **Hub pages: Standard | Wide | Theatre, Wide default, sidebar in Standard** (2026-10-08, `main_js/hub-theatre.js` / `.css`): the pages that only had Standard | Theatre now get the sidebar-page switch. `hub-theatre.js` injects `<aside id="mes-aside">` (family from the path, `familyOf()`), marks `#outer-container` `.has-aside` and loads `aside.css` / `aside.js`; Standard = content + recommendations column, Wide and Theatre = one column with the cards as a grid underneath (`html.hub-sb` rules at the end of `hub-theatre.css`; "Show sidebar" brings the column back). The `HUB-THEATRE-HEAD` snippet (every hub page, the generators, `add_hub_theatre.py`) defaults `pageMode` to `wide` and restores `asideHidden` / `asideSide`. Livestreams, the homepage and bare `.container` shells keep the two-state switch.

- **Sidebar flip on Jump-to pages, timer mute, clock full-screen controls** (2026-10-09): (1) the "More like this" rail on Jump-to pages (`jump-aside.js` builds its markup) now carries the same ⇄ flip button as the other sidebars (aside.js wires it; `jump-aside.css` shows it), so the side can be switched from either the Jump-to list or the rail (one shared `asideSide`). (2) Timer Display tiles have a speaker button next to the tab pin: `S.muted` (list of item keys `t:id` / `s:id` / `a:id` / `po` / `iv`, saved with the timer state) silences the ring / interval cues of that item only (banner still shows; the alarm-group tile mutes all its alarms at once). (3) Clock full screen: a control strip (Analog / Digital / Both, 12/24 h, Sec, Date, Theme) next to Close, auto-fading after 4 s idle; in landscape with Both, the face sits left and time + date right (`.ck-both` + grid) instead of the digital part being pushed off-screen.

- **Fluid Simulator** (2026-10-09, `mes.fm/fluid-simulator`, ocean blue `#0369a1`; `/navier-stokes`, `/fluid`, `/fluids`, `/fluid-simulation`, `/cfd`, `/vortex-street` 308 to it; card in `tools.html` new "Simulations" category) -- a 2D incompressible Navier-Stokes solver running live in the browser, built by `build_tool_apps.py` from `tool_apps_src/fluid-simulator/` (`lib.js` solver + scenarios, `app.js` UI, `app.css`, `content.html`; wide page like the graphing calculators, so not in `add_sidebar.py`; has the bottom ad). **Solver** (`MESFluid.Fluid`, pure, node-testable: `node tool_apps_src/fluid-simulator-tests.js`, 18 checks): collocated grid with a ghost ring, y-up storage, "stable fluids" step = buoyancy + optional vorticity confinement -> advect velocity -> implicit (Gauss-Seidel) viscosity -> pressure projection (red-black SOR on the 5-point Laplacian, warm-started, solids excluded = Neumann, outflow = Dirichlet, periodic ghosts wrapped) -> advect dye (3 channels) and temperature. Advection is MacCormack with min/max clamping and a **gain of 0.75** (`macGain`; at 1.0 the scheme gains energy in smooth inviscid flow, 0.9 already blows up Taylor-Green; do not raise it). Units inside: cells and steps; `nu = U * L / Re` (`nuFor`). Boundary modes: `tunnel` (inflow left with a coloured dye rake, outflow right, free-slip top / bottom), `box` (no-slip, optional moving lid), `periodic`. Obstacles: `setShape` (cylinder, square, plate at an angle, wedge, tandem, side-by-side pair) + user-drawn walls (`paintSolid`). Verified: the cylinder at Re 200 sheds at **St = 0.20** (probe sign changes -> `period()`), Taylor-Green decay is within ~25% of the exact `exp(-4 nu k^2 t)` (numerical diffusion makes it a bit faster; the page plots both and says so), the lid-driven cavity circulates with the lid. Speed: ~6 ms/step at 192x96, ~10 ms at 256x128; the frame loop runs as many steps as fit in ~14 ms. Scenarios = `PRESETS` in `lib.js` (13: 7 tunnel, cavity, stir, plume, Kelvin-Helmholtz, vortex merger, Taylor-Green); the "Try this" chips are `TRY` in `app.js`. UI: Smoke / Spin (vorticity) / Speed / Pressure views + arrows, Stir / Heat / Wall / Erase brushes, Reynolds slider (log, 10-5000), wind speed, swirl boost, buoyancy, obstacle shape / size / tilt, grid detail, share link (`?p=&re=&u=&v=&res=&ob=&sz=&ang=&vort=&beta=&a=`), localStorage `mes-fluid-simulator:v1` (preset, detail, speed, view, arrows, options). Logos are PIL placeholders (`fluid-simulator/img/logo.png`, `logo-big.png` 1200x630, `img/fluid-simulator-logo.png`) until Grok art lands. After editing run `build_tool_apps.py --only=fluid-simulator --apply`, `add_tool_page_controls.py --apply`, `add_bottom_ad.py --apply`, `add_cross_links.py --apply` (each rewrites other tool pages: `git checkout` the churn), then `build_sitemap.py` and `build_search_index.py`. Not done yet: 3D version, Reynolds-number calculator page, exact-solution explorer.

- **Atomic Clock Simulator** (2026-10-09, `mes.fm/atomic-clock-simulator`, blue `#1e4fb3`; `/atomic-clock`, `/atomic-clocks`, `/cesium-clock`, `/caesium-clock` 308 to it, `/clock-drift`, `/clock-drift-calculator`, `/ramsey-fringes`, `/bloch-sphere`, `/fountain-clock`, `/allan-deviation` to its tabs via `?tab=drift|fringe|bloch|fountain|stab`; card in `tools.html` "Simulations" and `calculators.html` "Physics & Science"; tiles on `/science` (also Fluid, Photoelectric, Reynolds) and `/math` (Sum of Integers, Fluid). Wide page like the Fluid Simulator (own Theatre button, bottom ad, no sidebar), built by `build_tool_apps.py` from `tool_apps_src/atomic-clock-simulator/`. `lib.js` (`MESAtomic`, pure, `node tool_apps_src/atomic-clock-simulator-tests.js`, 70 checks) = the exact two-level Rabi and Ramsey line shapes (fringe FWHM = 1/(2T), first minimum at 1/(2T)), the locked-clock law sigma_y = (1/pi)(dnu/nu0)(1/SNR)sqrt(Tc/tau), a servo `Loop` (quartz random-walk + temperature kicks, integrator lock, capture range, `retune`), the seven-clock ladder `CLOCKS` (quartz, TCXO, Rb, Cs beam, H maser, Cs fountain, Sr optical: ballpark accuracy `acc`, 1 s noise `a`, flicker `floor` from data sheets / NIST-JILA papers, not metrology), a deterministic clock race, Allan deviation of a series, and the drift maths / parsers (`5 ppm`, `2e-9`, `10 ns`) / formatters. Seven tabs: How it works (live loop, resonance, strip chart; Lock / Warm the room / Re-tune), Fountain & fringes (T, SNR, probe detuning, single-pass comparison), **Bloch sphere (3D)** (plain-canvas sphere you drag to rotate: `blochPath()` in lib.js rotates the state vector about W = (omega, 0, delta) during the pi/2 pulses and (0, 0, delta) during the wait, and is tested to equal `ramseyP` at both detuning signs; animated pulse 1 / wait / pulse 2 with scrub, step chips, detuning presets and the fringe marker; T is shared with the fountain tab), **3D fountain** (plain-canvas 3D scene you drag to rotate: tube, six cooling lasers, cavity, detection sheet and 260 atoms from a seeded Gaussian; `fountainGeometry()` gives the ballistic parabola z(t) = -d + v_l t - g t^2/2 through the cavity at t1 and t1+T, apex g T^2/8, lateral spread sigma_v = sqrt(kB Temp / m), and the fraction of atoms still inside the 1 cm cavity opening on return; atoms that miss it turn red and fade, and `fountainRelStability` shows why a taller throw stops helping unless the atoms are colder; atom temperature slider 0.2-50 uK in localStorage `temp`, cloud drawn 6x wider than true; the microwave offset comes from the Fringes tab), Clock race (speed up to 100 years per second), Stability chart (log-log, hover), Drift calculator (fraction <-> seconds per period, time until off by X, nearest clock, light-travel distance, caesium cycles, shareable `?tab=drift&f=&t=&per=`). State localStorage `mes-atomic-clock-simulator:v1`. Art = Grok (`atomic-clock-simulator/img/logo.png` 512 rounded corners, `logo-big.png` 1200x630 = the share image fitted on a blurred copy of itself, `img/atomic-clock-simulator-logo.png` 352, science tile `img/atomic-clock-simulator-icon.jpg`); `~/Downloads/atomic-clock-simple.jpg` (plain clock) is unused. Gotcha: rAF does not run in a hidden Browser pane, so to test the loop there override `requestAnimationFrame` with a timeout and re-eval the script. After editing run `build_tool_apps.py --only=atomic-clock-simulator --apply`, `add_tool_page_controls.py --apply`, `add_bottom_ad.py --apply`, `add_cross_links.py --apply` (each rewrites other tool pages: `git checkout` the churn).

- **3D Fluid Simulator** (2026-10-09, `mes.fm/3d-fluid-simulator`, cyan `#0e7490`; `/fluid-3d`, `/3d-fluid`, `/navier-stokes-3d`, `/fluid-simulator-3d`, `/3d-navier-stokes` 308 to it; card in `tools.html` "Simulations"; linked from the 2D simulator) -- built by `build_tool_apps.py` from `tool_apps_src/3d-fluid-simulator/` (`lib.js`, `app.js`, `app.css`, `content.html`; wide page like the 2D one: own Theatre button, no sidebar, bottom ad). **Physics is on the CPU** (`MESFluid3.Fluid3`, pure, `node tool_apps_src/3d-fluid-simulator-tests.js`, 11 checks): 3D stable fluids on a grid whose outermost layer is the boundary (cell types 0 fluid / 1 wall or obstacle / 2 outflow / 3 inflow), semi-Lagrangian advection (no MacCormack: too slow in 3D, so no explicit viscosity and no Reynolds slider), vorticity confinement, buoyancy, pressure projection by red-black SOR (20 iterations, omega 1.7, Neumann at solids, Dirichlet p=0 at outflow). Speed ~3 ms/step at 24x36x24, 7 ms at 32x48x32, 14 ms at 40x60x40, 25 ms at 48x72x48; the frame loop runs steps for up to 14 ms per frame at 30 steps/s x speed. **Rendering is WebGL2 only for display**: `pack()` writes one RGBA8 3D texture (R smoke, G temperature, B colour tag, A = speed/2 or 255 for real obstacles; the box walls are NOT packed solid or the whole box renders as an opaque block, found the hard way) and a fragment shader ray-marches it (110 steps, 64 on touch devices, one shadow sample per step, hot smoke tinted orange, Colours / Speed palettes, obstacles shaded from the alpha gradient, box edges). Orbit camera (drag / wheel / pinch, double-click resets, Auto-rotate), Stir tool (drag pushes the smoke on a plane through the box centre, `rayAt()`), no WebGL2 -> a flat top-down canvas projection + a note. Scenarios (`PRESETS` in lib.js): rising plume (heater + buoyancy, open top), smoke ring cannon and two colliding rings (`fire()`: an impulsive push through a round orifice rolls up into a vortex ring; auto-fire option, key F), wind past a sphere / cube (tunnel, dotted smoke rake, smoke fades by default there), stir tank (layered smoke). Detail 24 / 32 / 40 / 48 cells on the short side, share link `?p=&res=&view=&dens=&beta=&vort=&u=&ob=`, localStorage `mes-3d-fluid-simulator:v1`. Checked in the preview pane by clicking Step (the pane is hidden so rAF never runs: the page skips frames while `document.hidden`). Logos are PIL placeholders (`3d-fluid-simulator/img/logo.png`, `logo-big.png`, `img/3d-fluid-simulator-logo.png`) until Grok art lands. - **Electromagnetic Fields Simulator** (2026-10-12, `mes.fm/electromagnetic-fields-simulator`, burnt orange `#c2410c`; `/electromagnetism`, `/electric-field`, `/gauss-law`, `/amperes-law`, `/lorentz-force`, `/cyclotron`, `/mass-spectrometer`, `/maxwell-equations`, `/em-waves`, `/fdtd` ... 308 to it; card in `tools.html` "Simulations"). Built by `build_tool_apps.py` from `tool_apps_src/electromagnetic-fields-simulator/` (`lib.js` + `app.js` + `app.css` + `content.html`); normal-width page in the tools sidebar collection, `em` prefix in `stage-ctl.js` (all five boxes are free-shape: `data-mes-free` + `MESStage.sh`), equations via `mes-math.js`. `lib.js` (`MESEM`, pure, `node tool_apps_src/electromagnetic-fields-simulator-tests.js`, 49 checks, SI units): `fieldAt` / `potentialAt` / `forceOn` for point charges (1/r^2) or infinite line charges (1/r), `traceLine` + `fieldLines` (RK4 along E from the positive charges, a count proportional to |q|), `contours` (marching squares, grid sampled once for many levels), **`gauss`** (3D: flux through a sphere by 90 x 180 quadrature including the out-of-plane field; 2D: circle per metre; both verified against q_enc / eps0 to < 0.2% and zero for charges outside), `stepFree` (adaptive leapfrog for a free charge, energy conserved), wires perpendicular to the plane (`wireB` = mu0 I / 2 pi r, `wireA` vector potential so field lines = contours of A_z, `ampere` loop integral, `wireForce` per metre: 2e-7 N/m for 1 A at 1 m, i.e. the definition of the ampere), **`borisStep`** (Boris pusher on u = gamma v, relativistic option; conserves energy to rounding error, E x B drift = E/B, velocity selector straight, relativistic radius gamma m v / qB), and **`Yee`** (2D TMz FDTD, c = 1, cell = 1, S = 0.5: Ez / Hx / Hy, per-cell epsilon_r, conductivity and PEC mask, 18-cell cubic matched sponge with electric and magnetic loss, soft point / line sources with pulse envelopes, time-averaged intensity; checked: pulse speed c, speed c/n in epsilon_r = 4 by cross-correlation, closed PEC cavity keeps its energy and rings at the analytic (1,1) frequency, absorbing edges leave < 5% of the energy, PEC reflects). **Charges tab update (2026-10-13):** field lines now start from BOTH signs (negative charges trace backwards), skipping seeds inside neighbouring charges, so the underside of a stacked dipole is no longer empty; arrows default on (direction arrows, uniform colour); sensors (📍 tool: yellow dot, E arrow, |E| and angle label, draggable); Reset (back to the loaded example) and Clear buttons in the stage; tape measure (drag, distance label) and voltmeter (two probes, shows ΔV; click twice, drag to move); Backspace/Delete removes the selected charge or sensor (document-level, tip line under the tools). Five tabs (`?tab=es|ms|pt|wv|pw`): **Electric charges** (add / move / delete, field lines, arrows, equipotentials, potential or |E| colour map, Gauss surface with live numeric flux vs Q/eps0, drop test charges that fall under Coulomb force, pointer readout of E and V, force on the selected charge, 7 examples, point or line charges, share link), **Currents** (wires out / in, field lines from A_z contours, arrows, |B| map, Ampere loop with live integral vs mu0 I_enc, force per metre on the selected wire, solenoid and coax examples), **Charged particle** (proton / electron / alpha / muon / C-12,13,14 ions in uniform B out of the page and E, relativistic auto / always / never, radius / period / drift readouts with a measured period, presets: cyclotron, electron, E x B cycloid, velocity selector with 3 speeds, mass spectrometer with 3 isotopes, 0.9 c proton, muon), **Waves** (240 x 140 grid, tools glass / metal / absorber / erase / source / probe, 11 scenes: plane wave, mirror, glass slab, prism, lens, double / single slit, waveguide, dipole antenna, cavity, absorber; Ez or time-average intensity view, Poynting arrows, probe scope, wavelength and scale selector so 1 wavelength = 3 m / 12.2 cm / 10 um / 550 nm gives the real frequency) and **Plane wave** (3D picture of E and B for linear / circular / elliptical polarisation, any wavelength 0.1 nm to 1 km with the band name, frequency, B = E/c, intensity, photon energy). Limits are on the page: 2D single-polarisation FDTD (no Brewster angle), ~1% edge reflection, numerical dispersion below ~10 cells per wavelength. Art is Grok (2026-10-13; tile on `/science`). Rebuild: `build_tool_apps.py --only=electromagnetic-fields-simulator --apply` + the usual four follow-ups (`git checkout` the churn).
- **Audit of the simulators** (2026-10-12, in progress; the user wants universities to be able to rely on them): every control of each page was swept by a script (all ranges at min / mid / max, every select option, checkboxes, buttons, no console errors, no NaN / undefined in the page text) and the physics spot-checked in the browser against known answers. **Done and clean:** Fluid (cylinder Re 200 St = 0.218, Taylor-Green, cavity, all presets), 3D Fluid, Light in a Medium, Black Hole (Sun rs 2.953 km, photon sphere 4.43 km, shadow 7.67 km, ISCO 8.86 km, density, Hawking T, tides, deflection angles), Mandelbrot (point calculator orbits, period-2 and period-3 points, seahorse valley render), Metric Tensor (all 16 presets against textbook results: S3 R = 6, Ellis R = -2b^2/(b^2+l^2)^2, FLRW matter R = 4/(3t^2), K = 80/(27 t^4), RN K = 8(6M^2r^2 - 12MQ^2 r + 7Q^4)/r^8, Schwarzschild-dS R = 4 Lambda, torus K = 4 cos^2/(r^2 (R + r cos)^2), ...), Orbit (launch by drag gives e = 0 / 0.75 / 0.44 for 1 / 0.5 / 1.2 x circular speed, calculator vs ISS / GPS / GEO / Moon / Hohmann), Electromagnetic (Gauss 0.01% off, Ampere exact, particle presets). **Bugs found and fixed:** double pendulum drag measured the angle from straight UP (a ball dragged below the pivot jumped to its mirror image), small exponents printed as `10−⁴` (minus not superscript) in Orbit and Double Pendulum, `stage-ctl` box shrinking on FTL / Solar System Today. **Audit complete 2026-10-13 (desktop + 375px mobile):** every simulator, calculator and tool swept (all controls, no JS errors / NaN), physics checked against known values, all node + CAS/metric suites pass. Mobile: no page-level horizontal scroll anywhere; wide tables / MathJax boxes scroll inside their own wrappers. `stage-ctl.js` now hides the Small/Medium/Large presets at <=700px (Fill window stays), makes bar buttons 36px tall and the corner grip 32px on coarse pointers. Not done: shared `_shared.css` tap-size rule (a full rebuild churns ~70 unrelated pages). Known limits: Mandelbrot 4e-14 zoom, Schwarzschild-only black hole, extensions untested in real Chrome.
- **Ground Rod & Earthing Simulator** (2026-10-14, `mes.fm/grounding-rod-simulator`, amber-brown `#a16207`; `/ground-rod`, `/grounding`, `/earthing`, `/step-voltage`, `/static-electricity` ... 308 to it; card in `tools.html` "Simulations"). Built by `build_tool_apps.py` from `tool_apps_src/grounding-rod-simulator/` (`lib.js` = `MESGnd`, pure, `node tool_apps_src/grounding-rod-simulator-tests.js`, 34 checks; `app.js`, `app.css`, `content.html`); normal-width page in the tools sidebar collection, bottom ad. Five tabs (`?tab=rod|fault|light|static|rules`, shared `?rho=&L=&d=&n=&s=&t=`): **Ground rod** (Dwight R = rho/(2 pi L)(ln(4L/a)-1); n rods in a line from a self + Sunde mutual resistance matrix solved for equal potential; rods needed for a target such as the US 25 ohm; length x count grid; 100 ohm-m, 8 ft x 5/8 in = 40.4 ohm), **Shock & faults** (hot-to-case fault with a proper EGC vs a rod only vs no ground: fault current, coarse breaker trip time, case voltage, body current on a 1 kohm body + floor; the point is that a rod alone gives ~2 A and never trips a breaker; GFCI/RCD note; TT rule RA x I_dn <= 50 V table), **Lightning** (ground rise I x R, step voltage rho I/(2 pi) (1/r - 1/(r+stride)), voltage between two unbonded rods, log-log canvas; point-source uniform-soil model, labelled as order of magnitude), **Static** (energy 1/2 C V^2, RC drain time, minimum-ignition-energy table; the earth resistance is irrelevant next to the path resistance), **Rules by region** (US NEC, Canada CEC, UK BS 7671, Australia/NZ AS/NZS 3000, Europe IEC 60364 / Germany, Vietnam TCVN 7447, general). The rules text was checked against secondary sources only (trade articles, WorkSafe NT bulletin): the Canadian rod spacing (3 m vs the older 2 m) and Vietnam's "about 4 ohm" are flagged on the page as unverified; the page tells readers to use a licensed electrician. Logos are PIL placeholders (`grounding-rod-simulator/img/logo.png` 512, `logo-big.png` 1200x630, `img/grounding-rod-simulator-logo.png` 352) until Grok art lands. - **Double Pendulum** (2026-10-12, `mes.fm/double-pendulum`, purple `#7e22ce`; `/pendulum`, `/chaos`, `/chaos-pendulum`, `/butterfly-effect`, `/lyapunov` ... 308 to it; card in `tools.html` "Simulations"). Built by `build_tool_apps.py` from `tool_apps_src/double-pendulum/` (`lib.js` + `app.js` + `app.css` + `content.html`); normal-width page in the tools sidebar collection (Wide default), bottom ad, `dp` prefix in `stage-ctl.js`. `lib.js` (`MESPend`, pure, `node tool_apps_src/double-pendulum-tests.js`, 24 checks): Lagrangian equations of motion for any masses / lengths / gravity / joint friction, fixed-step RK4 in place on `[th1, th2, w1, w2]` (angles from straight down, y up), `energy`, `positions`, `normalModes` (m1 l1 l2 w^4 - g(m1+m2)(l1+l2) w^2 + g^2(m1+m2) = 0 -> periods and arm ratios; the "Slow / Fast mode" examples start exactly on them), **`flipTime`** (time for the outer arm to pass over the top, from rest) and **`canFlip`**: a start released at rest can only flip if its potential energy is >= `flipEnergy` = -(m1+m2) g l1 + m2 g l2, which is exact, so those cells of the map are never simulated (and are the black region). Checked: energy drift 1e-6 over a chaotic minute at h = 2 ms, step convergence, normal-mode period closure, simple-pendulum limit, a 1e-9 rad difference reaching 1 rad (the butterfly effect), 60 x 60 map cells in ~0.5 s. UI: canvas with drag-a-ball start, trails (colour per copy), **copies** (1-60 pendulums starting 10^-9...10^-1 rad apart: 2 = twin, 30+ = rainbow fan), graphs (both angles; log distance between the copies with a Lyapunov exponent estimated from the time the distance grows from 10 d0 to ~0.05), energy-drift readout, flip counter, speed control in the picture (- / + and `[` `]` keys), presets, gravity chips, links `?a1=&a2=&m1=...&n=&eps=`; second tab = **chaos map** (every start pair of angles coloured by flip time on a log scale, progressive rendering in 14 ms chunks, 80-300 cells, 5-40 s window, click a point to watch that start, marker of the current start, PNG). The photo of the pendulum in the map is computed on the main thread (no worker). Share image = Grok (`logo-big.png`); the tile `img/double-pendulum-icon.jpg` is the tool's REAL flip-time chaos map rendered by node from `lib.js` (the Grok tile looked like a Mandelbrot set; the Grok icon drew three arms, so `logo.png` / `img/double-pendulum-logo.png` stay PIL placeholders until a two-arm icon arrives). Rebuild: `build_tool_apps.py --only=double-pendulum --apply` + the usual four follow-ups (`git checkout` the churn).
- **Equation boxes typeset with MathJax** (2026-10-12): the "key equations" boxes of the Black Hole, Metric Tensor, Orbit, Double Pendulum, Light in a Medium, Atomic Clock, Fluid and Mandelbrot pages were hand-made HTML (`<sub>` sizes differed, no spacing); they are now TeX in `\( \displaystyle ... \)` inside `<span class="xx-eq__f mes-tex">`, typeset by `mes.fm/main_js/mes-math.js` (MathJax 3.2.2 `tex-svg` from cdnjs, loaded after `load`, only on pages that have `.mes-tex`, only those elements processed); `_shared.css` has the layout rules. New page with equations: use the same markup and add `<script src="/main_js/mes-math.js" defer></script>` at the end of `content.html`.
- **Corner grip resizes both ways** (2026-10-12, `stage-ctl.js`): dragging the grip now sets width AND height (`state = {w, h}` in `mes-stage:<path>`). Simulators that lay themselves out for any shape declare it with `data-mes-free="1"` on their `.xx-wrap` and read their stage height through `MESStage.sh(stage, naturalHeight)` (an exact user height, or only a *cap* for the Small / Medium presets); today: Orbit, Double Pendulum (pendulum tab), Mandelbrot (explorer renders at the box's own aspect), Black Hole (light + orbit tabs), Metric Tensor (CSS: `height: var(--mes-sh, min(var(--mes-shmax,...), clamp(...)))`). Every other simulator keeps its picture's shape, so there the box is the largest one of that shape that fits inside the chosen width x height (stretching a fixed-shape picture would distort it and break pointer maths). A new free-shape simulator: set the attribute and call `MESStage.sh`.
- **stage-ctl glitches fixed** (2026-10-12, after the Shadow Simulator flickered / shrank): the Small / Medium cap on the stage height turns into a width cap through the stage's aspect ratio, which feeds back for a stage whose height does not follow its width (FTL Shadow: height follows the scene and is clamped; Solar System Today: a CSS height). A box could shrink to its 320 px floor or flicker between sizes. Now (1) `ftl-wrap` is exempt (`NOCAP`), (2) a stage whose height stays put when its width changes is detected and gets width caps only (`__fixedH`), and (3) a box whose max-width changes more than 6 times in 3 s is frozen on width-only caps (`__frozen`). Checked at 1700 px for all 13 simulator pages from a clean localStorage (all 900 px and stable). If a new simulator has odd stage sizing, test it at >= 1250 px with a cleared `mes-stage:` key.
- **Orbit Simulator** (2026-10-12, `mes.fm/orbit-simulator`, rust `#9a3412`; `/orbit`, `/orbits`, `/gravity-simulator`, `/n-body`, `/three-body-problem`, `/kepler`, `/keplers-laws`, `/hohmann-transfer`, `/gravity-assist`, `/slingshot` ... 308 to it; card in `tools.html` "Simulations"). Built by `build_tool_apps.py` from `tool_apps_src/orbit-simulator/` (`lib.js` + `app.js` + `app.css` + `content.html`); normal-width page in the tools sidebar collection (Wide default), bottom ad, `or` prefix in `stage-ctl.js`. `lib.js` (`MESOrbit`, pure, `node tool_apps_src/orbit-simulator-tests.js`, 49 checks): `Sim` = Newtonian N-body in 2D (O(n^2), **4th-order Yoshida leapfrog with an adaptive step = 0.03 x the shortest pair timescale** `min(sqrt(r^3/G(m1+m2)), r/v)`; optional softening; optional merge on touch conserving mass + momentum), `elements()` (a, e, T, rp, ra, periapsis angle, retrograde, bound / escape from the relative state), `primaryOf` (the body pulling hardest, so a moon is measured about its planet), `orbitPath` (ellipse / hyperbola points), `visViva`, `hohmann`, and 12 presets with their own unit systems (`SOL` = AU, years, solar masses, G = 4 pi^2; Earth-Moon in 10^5 km and days; figure-eight / Lagrange / Pythagorean with G = 1): Earth, inner planets, giant planets, Halley (retrograde, e 0.967), Jupiter's Trojans, Earth and Moon, figure-eight, Lagrange triangle, Pythagorean 3-4-5 problem, circumbinary planet, **gravity assist at Jupiter (probe tuned by a grid search: a 3.09 -> 6.0 AU, passes 0.013 AU from Jupiter)**, sandbox. Checked: Earth T = 1 yr and 10-year energy drift 5e-10, T^2/a^3 = 1 for every inner planet, Halley period 75.3 yr and constant angular momentum, Moon 27.3 d, figure-eight returns to 1e-8 after one period, Trojan stays near L4, Pythagorean energy within 5e-3 through its close encounters. `app.js`: canvas, drag from empty space to launch (**velocity = drag / distance-to-primary x circular speed**, with a live dashed predicted orbit and "k x circular" label), drag a body to move it, click to select, wheel / pinch zoom, right- / shift-drag pan, camera fixed on the centre of mass or following the selected body (trails are stored relative to the frame origin and cleared when the frame changes), velocity arrows, osculating orbit of the selected body with periapsis / apoapsis dots, **equal-area slices** (12 slices of T/12 for the selected bound body, area numbers in the note), merge option, trail length, time speed (log slider, units per second of the preset), new-body mass slider, bodies table with e, period and T^2/a^3 (AU / years systems), energy drift readout (baseline reset when a body is added / removed / moved), PNG, fullscreen, share link `?s=<base64url of the whole system>` (`?p=<preset>` for a plain one); localStorage `mes-orbit-simulator:v1`. Second tab = SI **orbit calculator** (Earth, Moon, Sun, Mars, Venus, Mercury, Jupiter, Saturn or custom; circular orbit speed / period / escape speed / g, Kepler III both ways, vis-viva, Hohmann delta-v and time; inputs like `400 km`, `1 AU`). Not modelled: 3D, relativity, tides, rotating frames, non-point masses. Art is Grok (2026-10-12; tile on `/science`). Rebuild: `build_tool_apps.py --only=orbit-simulator --apply` + the usual four follow-ups (`git checkout` the churn).

Tests: known points in / out, c = -1 period 2, rabbit bulb period 3, area ~ 1.5066 from a pixel count, real-axis symmetry, smooth colouring, Julia z -> -z symmetry, tricorn / cubic symmetries, worker source works when rebuilt from text. `app.js`: a pool of up to 4 workers fills the picture in 4-8 row bands from the middle outwards into a Float32 buffer, so **palette, colour speed / shift and colour cycling recolour instantly without recomputing**; pan / wheel / pinch / double-click show the old picture transformed at once and re-render after you stop (a new picture is built off-screen and drawn over the old); Julia preview (220x124, main thread) and the orbit of the point under the pointer; click picks a point, "Open this point's Julia set" / "Back"; automatic iteration count from the zoom or a slider; Draft / Normal / Sharp quality; PNG, fullscreen; share link `?t=&x=&y=&s=&j=&jx=&jy=&i=&p=&tab=`; a Point calculator tab (any c = a + bi, escape step or cycle period, 40-step orbit table, "Show this point in the Explorer"). localStorage `mes-mandelbrot:v1`. **Limit: 64-bit doubles, so the width stops at 4e-14** (said on the page; deep zoom would need perturbation theory + arbitrary precision, not built). Checked: shadow 3 sqrt(3)/2 rs by bisection, weak-field 2 rs/b (+ 15 pi/16 b^-2), periapsis advance 3 pi / (2 L^2), ISCO stability, rs of Sun 2.95 km / Earth 8.9 mm, Hawking 61.7 nK. Four tabs (`?tab=light|orbit|image|numbers`, each with its own link keys): **Bending light** (top view, 5-61 rays, hover / drag chooses the highlighted ray b, Einstein vs Newton (half) bending, closest approach, photon sphere and shadow circles, optical-analogue overlay with a medium glow), **Orbits** (animated, Newtonian ellipse dashed, trail, clock on the particle vs a distant clock, measured vs weak-field precession, seven example chips), **The picture** (per-pixel ray trace: lensed procedural star sky (2048x1024 float texture, nearest sampling), thin disk 3 rs to the chosen edge with Doppler beaming / gravitational redshift / banding, three colour modes, inclination / distance / FOV, shadow-edge marker, PNG, fullscreen; progressive rendering in 14 ms chunks, 180 / 270 / 400 px wide) and **Numbers** (mass in M-sun / Earth masses / kg, seven preset objects, distance slider 1-1000 rs, tides for a body of chosen length). localStorage `mes-black-hole-simulator:v1`. Also added a `blackhole` scenario (n capped at 4) to Light in a Medium; both pages link each other. Not modelled: spin (Kerr), charge, thick or turbulent disks, light from the disk's own gravity, a moving sky. Logos are PIL placeholders (`black-hole-simulator/img/logo.png` 512, `logo-big.png` 1200x630, `img/black-hole-simulator-logo.png` 352) until Grok art lands (same file names). After editing run `build_tool_apps.py --only=black-hole-simulator --apply`, `add_tool_page_controls.py --apply`, `add_sidebar.py --apply`, `add_bottom_ad.py --apply`, `add_cross_links.py --apply` (each rewrites other tool pages: `git checkout` the churn), then `build_sitemap.py`, `build_search_index.py`, `build_aside_recs.py --apply`, `build_site_index.py --apply`, `build_redirects_index.py --apply`. - **Light in a Medium Simulator** (2026-10-12, `mes.fm/light-in-a-medium`, magenta `#be185d`; `/refraction`, `/snells-law`, `/snell`, `/refractive-index`, `/light-simulator`, `/light-in-a-medium-simulator`, `/slow-light`, `/speed-of-light-simulator`, `/wave-refraction` 308 to it; card in `tools.html` "Simulations"; first of three planned physics pages, then a Black Hole hub and a Metric Tensor Explorer).
