- **Michelson-Morley Experiment Simulator** (2026-10-09, `mes.fm/michelson-morley-experiment`, emerald `#047857`; `/michelson-morley`, `/michelson`, `/interferometer`, `/aether-wind` and a few more 308 to it; card in `tools.html` "Simulations" and `calculators.html` "Physics & Science", tile on `/science`; companion of the MES Math Q/A 57 Michelson-Morley page and the Aether series). Built by `build_tool_apps.py` from `tool_apps_src/michelson-morley-experiment/` (`lib.js` + `app.js` + `app.css` + `content.html`); wide page like the Atomic Clock / Fluid simulators (own Theatre button, bottom ad, no sidebar). `lib.js` (`MESMM`, pure, `node tool_apps_src/michelson-morley-tests.js`, 59 checks) uses the **exact** round-trip time in the aether frame, t = (2/c) sqrt(dx^2 + dy^2 - b^2 dy^2) / (1 - b^2) for an arm displacement (dx, dy) (dx along the motion), not the small-v approximation; N = 2 L v^2 / (lambda c^2) falls out (1887: 11 m, 570 nm, 29.78 km/s = 0.38 fringe). Models: stationary aether, aether + Lorentz-FitzGerald contraction (dx shrinks by sqrt(1 - b^2): exactly null at any angle / speed, tested), special relativity, emission theory (the last two just null), plus a "wind that reaches the lab" (drag) fraction for the aether model. Five tabs: Interferometer (top view on a turntable with wind streaks + live circular fringes, dashed rings mark the 0-degree bright fringes), Why the times differ (aether-frame animation with exaggerated beta, contraction checkbox), Turntable run (16 azimuths, N turns averaged, Gaussian noise, least-squares fit of cos 2th / sin 2th -> fitted wind or a 3-sigma upper limit), Real experiments (1881, 1887, 1902-04, Miller 1925, Joos 1930 with computed expected shifts; "Load this instrument"), Calculator (shift / wind speed / arm length, parses `11 m`, `2 ft`, `0.001 c`). The historical figures in `HISTORY` are as commonly cited (Joos's ~1.5 km/s and Miller's ~10 km/s especially): re-check them against the papers if the page is ever used as a reference. State localStorage `mes-michelson-morley-experiment:v1`; links `?tab=&m=&v=&L=&l=&felt=` and `?tab=calc&mode=&kL=&kl=&kv=&kN=`. Logos are PIL placeholders (`michelson-morley-experiment/img/logo.png` 512, `logo-big.png` 1200x630, `img/michelson-morley-experiment-logo.png` 352, `img/michelson-morley-experiment-icon.jpg` 900x600) until Grok art lands (same file names). After editing run `build_tool_apps.py --only=michelson-morley-experiment --apply`, `add_tool_page_controls.py --apply`, `add_bottom_ad.py --apply`, `add_cross_links.py --apply` (each rewrites other tool pages: `git checkout` the churn). Gotcha when testing in the Browser pane: the pane caches the page script, so fetch it with `{cache:'reload'}` (or bump `js_v`) after a rebuild.

- **Photo Editor** (2026-10-11, `mes.fm/photo` (`/photo-editor` 308s there; APPS key `photo`, `src="photo-editor"`), violet `#7c3aed`; `/photoeditor`, `/photo-edit`, `/image-editor`, `/online-photo-editor`, `/photo-editor-online`, `/photopea`, `/paint` 308 to it; card in `tools.html` new "Photos & Images" category; art = the user's Grok icon / share / tile: `photo-editor/img/logo.png` 512 rounded + `img/photo-editor-logo.png` 352 (square crop of the icon), `logo-big.png` 1200x630 (share image fitted on a blurred copy of itself), `img/photo-editor-icon.jpg` 900x600 tile (not used by a hub yet)). A browser-only layered editor aimed at replacing Gimp + Camtasia's image labelling: wide page, **always-dark workspace** (every colour in `app.css` is a `--pe-*` custom property so the derived TOOL-DARK block leaves it alone; `.pe *{color:inherit}` is needed because the shell's `*{color:#333}` otherwise paints every button grey), bottom ad, and (2026-10-15) a normal page in `add_sidebar.py`'s tools collection: Standard | Wide | Theatre + "More like this" sidebar (Wide by default), no `.outer-container` width override, and **no automatic Full window when an image is opened** (it hid the sidebar and the switch; the Full window button / key F and a remembered manual choice remain; the stage's ResizeObserver re-fits the picture when the width mode changes). Built by `build_tool_apps.py` from `tool_apps_src/photo-editor/` with `js_parts=["core.js","tools.js","tools2.js","dialogs.js"]` + `lib_from=["image-converter"]` (served as one `js/photo-editor.js`, order: MESImg, MESPixel `lib.js`, parts, `app.js`; the parts share the global `window.PE`).
  **`lib.js` (`MESPixel`, pure, `node tool_apps_src/photo-editor-tests.js`, 32 checks)** = levels / curves (monotone cubic LUT) / brightness-contrast / exposure / hue-sat / vibrance / temperature / invert / grayscale / sepia / threshold / posterize, gaussian + box blur (3 box passes, premultiplied alpha so edges do not fringe), unsharp, noise, pixelate, vignette, 3x3 convolution, flood-fill mask (scanline), grow / shrink / feather / invert / bounds of masks, `blendByMask`, auto levels.
  **`core.js`** = the model. A layer is a canvas plus a *non-destructive transform* (`cx, cy, sx, sy, rot`: move / scale / rotate / flip cost nothing); `PE.bake(L)` bakes it into pixels (and the mask) only when a pixel tool needs it, `PE.growLayer` extends a layer's canvas, `PE.prepare(L, quiet, noGrow)` = raster + baked + (unless `noGrow`) at least document-sized (paint tools grow, adjustments / effects do not, so a small pasted layer keeps its own size). Compositing draws every layer at the *view* resolution (a viewport-sized comp canvas, so a 24 MP photo costs the same to redraw as a small one) with native canvas blend modes; masked layers and layers with a live stroke go through a temp canvas. Selection = a document-size canvas (alpha = amount; modes new / add / subtract / intersect via composite ops; `PE.selMask()` caches the alpha plane; ants are rebuilt per view from screen pixels). **History = snapshots**: `PE.commit(label)` stores each layer's canvas by reference when its `rev` has not changed (so an edit costs one canvas), 100 steps / 700 MB budget, undo restores clones. Image-level ops (`cropTo`, `canvasSize`, `resizeImage`, `rotateImage`, `rotateArbitrary`, `flipImage`, `trim`) move the layers' transforms instead of resampling where possible. Text layers re-render via `PE.renderText` (font, size, align, line height, background box, outline, shadow; keeps the top-left corner fixed). Projects are stored-ZIP `.mesproj` (`project.json` + a PNG per layer / mask, `MESImg.zipStore` / `zipRead`); autosave to IndexedDB `mes-photo-editor` (key `auto`, 5 s after the last commit, offered as "Restore last session" in the empty state); PSD import / export via **ag-psd 30.1.1 from jsDelivr, loaded only when a PSD is opened or saved** (layers, opacity, visibility, blend modes; smart objects / styles / live text are flattened).
  **`tools.js` / `tools2.js`**: each tool is `PE.defTool({id, name, key, icon, opts, ui, down, move, up, overlay, ...})`; `ui` (option bar) is data-driven (`range`, `check`, `select`, `seg`, `color`, `number`, `button`, `showFor` / `hideFor` by `kind`, `show` = another option). Pointer dispatch handles pan (Space / middle / Hand), two-finger pinch + pan, pen pressure, coalesced events. Tools: Move + free transform (corner = uniform, Shift = free, outside a corner = rotate, snap to canvas edges; a full-canvas bottom layer is never auto-picked, `PE.isBackdrop`), Marquee (rect / ellipse, drag inside to move the selection), Lasso (freehand / polygon), Magic wand (layer or all layers, tolerance, contiguous, smooth), Crop (ratios, thirds grid), Brush / Eraser (strokes go to a layer-size buffer drawn per dab through the selection, committed at the end with the stroke opacity; on a layer mask the brush reveals and the eraser hides), Clone stamp (Alt-click source, aligned), Retouch (blur / sharpen / lighten / darken / saturate / desaturate via a pre-processed copy), Bucket, Gradient, Eyedropper, Text, **Shapes & annotations** (rect, rounded box, ellipse, line, arrow, callout bubble, numbered step badge with auto-increment, highlight (multiply), blur / pixelate / solid "hide area"; each lands on its own layer by default), Hand, Zoom. Dialogs: levels (histogram), curves, brightness / contrast, exposure, hue / saturation, vibrance, temperature, threshold, posterize, invert / black & white / sepia, make-a-colour-transparent, blur, unsharp, noise, pixelate, vignette, emboss, edges (all with live preview on a <=1100 px proxy, limited to the selection, params remembered), image / canvas size, new image presets, rotate / straighten, **export** (PNG / JPEG / WebP with live size estimate, scale, JPEG background; copy to clipboard), layer drop shadow / glow, outline, round corners, align, fit / fill canvas. **Opening several images (drop, paste, Open) adds them as layers**; paste of a clipboard image places it centred. The page switches itself to **Full window** on the first image (`S.win` in localStorage `mes-photo-editor:v1`; the floating compact bar otherwise covers the editor's top-right buttons). Gotchas: screenshots in the Browser pane lag one step behind the DOM; `PE.tmp(key, w, h)` returns a shared canvas, so every concurrent use needs its own key; `PE.restrictToSel` uses `selcrop`. **AI background removal** (2026-10-11, `ai.js`, Layer menu + a button in the Move tool's option bar): `PE.aiRemoveBackground()` runs **transformers.js 3.7.6** (jsDelivr, dynamic `import()` on first use) with two *permissively licensed* models downloaded from huggingface.co and cached by the browser (Cache API): **IS-Net general-use** `Ko033/isnet-general-use-onnx` (Apache-2.0; any object; WebGPU fp16 88 MB when the adapter has `shader-f16`, else CPU/wasm int8 46 MB; ~0.3 s on GPU, ~8 s on CPU for 1024 px) and **MODNet** `Xenova/modnet` (Apache-2.0; people, 6.6 MB wasm). The layer (transform baked, its mask ignored) is drawn to a canvas, the model returns a 1024 (IS-Net) / 512 (MODNet) float mask, min-max normalised, edge style Soft / Normal / Sharp (smoothstep), upscaled to the layer and applied as an **editable layer mask** (default; brush reveals, eraser hides), a permanent cut-out, or a selection ("Select the subject only"). Licences matter because the site has ads: **RMBG-1.4 / 2.0 are non-commercial and `onnx-community/ISNet-ONNX` is AGPL, so they are deliberately not used.** BiRefNet (MIT) was tried and does not run: its WebGPU kernels need more than 10 storage buffers per shader stage (Apple GPUs stop at 10; the failure shows up as `numbers_storage_buffers_ <=` or a bare number) and the wasm path hit the same provider; revisit if ORT-web gets a fallback. Gotchas: `RawImage.fromCanvas` is synchronous; a Python `http.server` preview caches the HTML, so after a rebuild fetch the page with `{cache:'reload'}` (or bump `js_v` and hard-reload). The shell helper that re-applies the page scripts must **not** `git checkout` the two tools' own folders (it reverted work once). **AI object erase** (2026-10-11, `erase.js`, tool `objerase`, key `J`, also Edit -> "Erase selection with AI"): paint over the object (Alt un-paints; marks are a red canvas shown on the overlay), then Enter / the Erase button (or "Erase when I release"); with no marks the selection is used. **LaMa** inpainting (Apache-2.0) as OpenCV's 92.6 MB ONNX (`huggingface.co/opencv/inpainting_lama`, derived from `Carve/LaMa-ONNX`; inputs `image` float 0..1 CHW and `mask` 0/1, both 512x512; output `output` 0..255 RGB), run through the shared onnxruntime-web helpers in `aiort.js` (WebGPU first, falls back to wasm on any error; LaMa ran on wasm here, ~14-20 s per run: wasm is single-threaded because the site is not cross-origin isolated; the model file is cached with the Cache API). Pipeline on the active layer (baked): bounding box of the marks -> a window with context (max side + 60 %, at least 512 px, clamped to the layer) -> `PE.resample` to 512 -> mask grown 2 px -> LaMa -> resampled back -> only the marked area (grown 2 px, feathered 3 px) is copied over the original, so every other pixel is untouched; one undo step. Large marked areas are filled at reduced resolution (window > 512 px), so the fill is softer than the photo; small objects on plain or repeating backgrounds work best. **LaTeX equations** (2026-10-11, `equation.js`, Equation tool `Q`, File -> "Insert equation (LaTeX)...", double-click an equation with the Move tool, or paste LaTeX text with Ctrl+V): MathJax **3.2.2 `tex-svg` from cdnjs** (the same version as `mes.fm/latex`; loaded on first use with `svg.fontCache: "none"` so the SVG has no `<use>` references), typeset at the requested pixel size (`em` / `ex` options), `currentColor` replaced by the chosen colour, rasterised at **2x** onto a transparent canvas (optional rounded background box with padding) and placed as a layer with scale 1/2, so it is crisp when enlarged. Invalid TeX is reported from the `data-mjx-error` attribute (no layer is made). The source and options live on `L.eq` (`tex, size, color, display, bgOn, bg, pad, radius, k, nw`; `nw` = natural visual width, used to keep a user's scaling when the equation is re-rendered), carried through history snapshots, `.mesproj` and duplicate; **painting on or adjusting the layer sets `eq = null`** (`PE.prepare`), AI upscaling scales equation layers' transform instead of running the model. Dialog: monospace TeX box, snippet chips (fraction, root, integral, sum, limit, partial, vector, matrix, cases, aligned, greek, boxed), size / colour / display style / background box, live preview, Ctrl+Enter inserts; the last settings are remembered (`mes-photo-editor:eq`).
**Layer groups + comps** (2026-10-11; the user makes many thumbnails of math videos from one Camtasia project full of groups, so this is the editor equivalent): a **group** is a header layer (`type "group"`, 1x1 canvas) stored in the flat array *after* its members; members point at it with `group` (its id) and are always contiguous, nesting allowed (`PE.relink()` sets `L._p`; `drawLayer` multiplies opacity / honours visibility up the chain, so group opacity is a per-member multiply, not a true group composite; group blend mode is ignored). `PE.groupLayers / ungroup / relocate(src, target, "above"|"below"|"into") / stepLayer / duplicateLayer / deleteLayer` keep the invariant; `PE.translate` moves a group's members; the Move tool picks the whole group (option "Pick the whole group") unless you are already inside it, groups show a dashed box instead of handles, align and nudge work on groups. Layers panel = tree (indent, collapse, count, drag zones: top = above, middle of a group = into, bottom = below), Ctrl / Shift-click multi-select, Ctrl+G / Ctrl+Shift+G, a find-a-layer box. **Comps** (`PE.comp*`, saved in `doc.comps`, part of history snapshots and `.mesproj` v2): a comp stores every layer's visibility, centre, scale, rotation, opacity and blend mode; layers that did not exist when it was saved are hidden when it is applied. Comps tab (next to History): + Save current view, click to show (active one outlined when the current state matches), update / rename / reorder / delete; `,` and `.` step through them; **Export all** (`PE.dlgExportComps`, also File menu) renders each ticked comp (same size or a fixed width) to PNG / JPEG / WebP, names via `{project}-{n}-{comp}`, one ZIP, and restores the current view afterwards. Gotcha: after undo / redo / project load the layer *objects* are new (ids persist), so never keep layer references across history steps. Not built yet: true group opacity / blend, group scale / rotate, masks on groups, comps that store z-order.
**Shared ONNX helpers** (`aiort.js`, `PE.aiRt`): onnxruntime-web 1.22.0 `ort.all.min.js` from jsDelivr, model download with progress + Cache API (`mes-photo-editor-models`), sessions that try WebGPU then fall back to wasm on a create *or run* failure (object erase now uses these). **AI upscaling** (2026-10-11, `upscale.js`, Image -> "Upscale with AI (2x / 4x)..."): **Real-ESRGAN general x4v3** (SRVGGNetCompact, BSD-3-Clause, 4.9 MB, `CoderViking/realesr-general-x4v3-onnx`; input `input` float 0..1 NCHW, output `output` x4). Every non-text layer (and its mask) is tiled (256 px on WebGPU, 128 px on wasm, 16 px context discarded), each tile goes through the x4 net and its centre is drawn at x4, or scaled to x2 with high-quality smoothing (so memory stays bounded); alpha is resampled separately; text layers re-render at the new size; the document grows by the factor (limit 16384 px / 120 MP) in one undo step, cancellable between tiles. Measured: ~0.3 s per 256 px tile on WebGPU, ~0.9 s per 128 px tile on wasm (a 600x800 result took ~8 s here). Rejected: Swin2SR (Xenova) -- 14 s per 128 px tile on wasm and its WebGPU build fails on a Slice kernel; Real-ESRGAN x4plus (67 MB) is far slower on the CPU. Not built yet: paths / pen, smudge, healing brush, liquify / warp, perspective, layer groups, adjustment layers, face restoration. After editing run `build_tool_apps.py --only=photo-editor,image-converter --apply`, `add_tool_page_controls.py --apply`, `add_sidebar.py --apply` (converter only), `add_bottom_ad.py --apply` and `git checkout` the churn those scripts cause on other pages.
- **Bulk Image Converter** (2026-10-11, `mes.fm/image-converter`, teal `#0f766e`; `/png-to-jpg`, `/png-to-jpeg`, `/jpg-to-png`, `/jpeg-to-png`, `/webp-to-png`, `/webp-to-jpg`, `/png-to-webp`, `/jpg-to-webp` redirect with `?to=jpg|png|webp`, `/imageconverter`, `/convert-images`, `/bulk-image-converter`, `/image-convert` plain; card in `tools.html` "Photos & Images"; normal-width page in the tools sidebar collection, bottom ad). Drop / pick / paste many images (or a folder) -> PNG, JPEG or WebP with quality, resize (max width / height / box / percent, never enlarges, halving steps when shrinking by more than 2x), background colour for transparent pixels going to JPEG, name pattern (`{name}`, `{n}`, `{w}`, `{h}` or a suffix), per-file download and one **ZIP** (store-only writer in `lib.js`, checked against Python's `zipfile`). Re-converts automatically (350 ms debounce) when a setting changes; two files at a time. `lib.js` (`MESImg`: `FORMATS`, `fitSize`, `uniqueName`, `outName`, `formatBytes`, `crc32`, `zipStore` / `zipRead`) is node-tested (`node tool_apps_src/image-converter-tests.js`, 13 checks) and shared with the photo editor through `lib_from`. HEIC only decodes in Safari (said on the page). Art = the user's Grok icon (2026-10-11), cropped to a true square inside its rounded shape and re-rounded (`image-converter/img/logo.png` 512, `img/image-converter-logo.png` 352); `logo-big.png` 1200x630 is that icon beside the title on teal (made with PIL, replace when a real share image exists).

- **MES browser extensions** (2026-10-11; hub `mes.fm/browser`, tiles for ours, other developers' extensions as simple links; homepage tile after Mobile Apps). Five plain Manifest V3 extensions, each with its source served as-is in `mes.fm/<slug>/extension/` (readable on the site), a download page built by `build_tool_apps.py` from `tool_apps_src/<slug>/` (normal-width tool page: sidebar, Standard | Wide | Theatre, bottom ad, TOOL-DARK; `app.js` only fills in the version from the extension's manifest), a packaging script `build_<name>_extension.py` (dry-runs by default, `--apply`: makes `icons/icon16|32|48|128.png` from `mes.fm/<slug>/img/logo.png` and zips to `mes.fm/<slug>/mes-<name>.zip`; **bump `version` in `extension/manifest.json` for a release**) and pure-logic tests `node tool_apps_src/<name>-tests.js`. All are installed unpacked (Developer mode -> Load unpacked) until a Chrome Web Store listing exists (deliberately deferred: dogfood first, then a listing kit); never loaded as a real extension in the dev environment, only exercised with a mocked `chrome` API in the Browser pane. Registered like every tool: `add_tool_page_controls` TOOLS, `add_cross_links` / `build_search_index` TOOL_DIRS, `organize_hub_cards` media list + a card in `tools.html`, `add_sidebar` tools collection, `add_bottom_ad` TOOL_DIRS. **Pages under any `extension/` folder are skipped by `build_sitemap.py` / `build_site_index.py`** and the popup / options HTML carry `noindex`.
  - **Video Speed Controller** (`/videospeed`, `build_video_speed_extension.py`, `videospeed-tests.js`, orange `#d9480f`): content script on all sites; keys D / S / R / G / Z / X / V / M / J; on-video pill (press-and-hold the label = temporary 2x, drag to change); *setups* (profiles) = a list of bindings (action, key, amount) + preferences (remember speed, hide by default, exclusive keys, audio, force) with built-ins "Default" and "MES's setup" (the maintainer's own: [ ] 0.05, Z / X 1 s, Y reset, P 2x, no hide-by-default); popup switcher, per-site setup, export / import.
  - **Read Aloud** (`/read-aloud`, `build_read_aloud_extension.py`, `read-aloud-tests.js`, green `#15803d`): injected on demand only (activeTab + scripting, no all-sites permission); `reader.js` finds the article text (or the selection) into a char -> DOM position map (`MESRA.buildText`), speaks one sentence at a time with the browser voices, highlights word + sentence with the CSS Custom Highlight API, floating player (draggable by its grip, minimise to a bar, highlight on / off (H), M, Space, arrows, Esc, double-click a word to jump); reuses the Speed Reader's `tool_apps_src/speedreader/lib.js` (copied to `extension/srlib.js` by the build script). Setups Default / Study / Quick listen. **The same engine runs on mes.fm itself:** `build_site_read_aloud.py --apply` generates `mes.fm/main_js/site-read-aloud.js` (reader + shared + srlib behind a localStorage stand-in for `chrome.storage`; re-run it after editing the extension's `reader.js` / `shared.js` or the Speed Reader's lib) and `main_js/site-search.js` adds a speaker button left of the search button in the header controls (only when `speechSynthesis` exists; the ~57 KB engine downloads on first click; `window.__mesraRoots` / `__mesraSite` are the two hooks).
  - **Google Docs** (Read Aloud 1.6.1, 2026-10-13): Docs draws its pages on a canvas, so the page has no text nodes and nothing used to be read. On `docs.google.com/document/d/<id>` `reader.js` now fetches the document's own plain-text export (`/document/d/<id>/export?format=txt`, same origin, with the person's login; `RA.docsId` / `RA.docsItems` in `shared.js`, tested) and reads that, one node-less item per paragraph. Nothing can be highlighted inside Docs, so the player's text box follows the words ("Text of this Google Doc ..."); the whole document is read (a selection in Docs is not visible to the page). Needs File > Download not to be disabled. Untested against a live Doc in the dev environment.
  - **Dark Mode** (`/dark-mode`, `build_dark_mode_extension.py`, `dark-mode-tests.js`, indigo `#4338ca`): content script in every frame at `document_start`; *smart invert* = one stylesheet (`invert(1) hue-rotate(180deg)` on `html`, photos / video / canvas / iframes / `[data-mesdm-img]` CSS-background photos flipped back, brightness / contrast / sepia / grayscale tweaks) added only when the wanted theme (setup mode dark / light / system / schedule, or the site's own Auto / Dark / Light / Off) differs from how the page is painted (median lightness of six sampled points); the last verdict per host is cached in `chrome.storage.local` (`mesdmSeen`) so a dark site is not flashed light. Alt+Shift+D flips the current site. Setups Dark / Follow system / Night / Paper. Honest limit: not per-element colour rewriting, so brand hues shift; a dynamic engine could be a later option.
  - **Volume Booster** (`/volume-booster`, `build_volume_booster_extension.py`, `volume-booster-tests.js`, amber `#b45309`): made for video playback (YouTube etc.) beyond the site's own limit. **Video mode (default, 1.1.0):** `content.js` + `shared.js` are injected on demand into every frame of the active tab (activeTab + scripting, no all-sites permission; the popup / shortcut drive it through `chrome.scripting.executeScript` funcs on `window.__mesvb`) and route the page's `<video>` / `<audio>` through `MESVB.buildGraph` (gain + optional **soft clipper** WaveShaper; a DynamicsCompressor was rejected because Chrome adds make-up gain, so the shown percentage would be wrong). Only blob: / data: / same-origin / `crossorigin` sources are routed (a cross-origin file would be silenced for good), a suspended AudioContext is reported not connected, nothing is touched at 100%, videos that start later join. **Whole tab mode (fallback):** `chrome.tabCapture` stream id -> an **offscreen document** plays the tab's audio through the same graph (shows Chrome's recording dot; needs Chrome 116+). Badge text, Alt+Shift+Up / Down, remembered level per site. The tabCapture glue and the real popup cannot be tested outside a real extension: video mode's routing was checked in a page with spies on Web Audio, the graph with an OfflineAudioContext (`0.2 x 600% = 1.2` raw, `0.906` limited).
  - **Text Size** (`/text-size`, `build_text_size_extension.py`, `text-size-tests.js`, teal `#0e7490`): content script scales *text only*: each text-bearing element gets inline `font-size` (+ px `line-height`) = original x level (`!important`, originals and the page's own inline values remembered, so 100% restores exactly); tracked ancestors are un-scaled while a new element's size is read so em / % / inherited sizes are never scaled twice; chunked on idle + MutationObserver. Levels 50-300% per site or for all sites, Alt+Shift+Up / Down / 0.
  - Short links: `/videospeed-extension`, `/speech-extension`, `/volume-extension` now redirect to our pages; the Chrome Web Store originals live at `/video-speed-controller`, `/read-aloud-original`, `/volume-booster-original`; plus aliases (see the `redirects` list in `vercel.json`).
  - **Shared-tree gotcha:** uncommitted edits to *tracked* files get reverted by another session's cleanup (it cost a full rewrite once, and a first commit silently lacked its redirects / hub card): commit right after every write and verify the diff of what you think you committed.
- `add_browser_extensions_nav.py` -- the **burger / side navbar** (the `navbar__item` list: Home, Math Tutorials, Calculators, Tools, ..., shared by the "Site Navigation" dropdown and the burger overlay) gets `Browser Extensions` (-> `/browser`) right after the flat Tools item on all ~1,350 pages that carry it (2026-10-11), plus the generators / templates that emit it (`tool_page_template.html`, `hive_mirror_template.html`, `math_qa_mirror_template.html`, `cubic-formula/child-template.html`, 17 `build.mjs`) so rebuilds keep it, and `percentagecalculator-app/build-www.py` strips it from the app build (like the Tools item). The horizontal info bar and the floating compact bar (`compact-nav-links`) are deliberately NOT touched: they are crowded and `main.js` highlights the active info-bar tab by position (`:eq(N)`). Idempotent; **dry-runs by default (`-v` lists files), `--apply` writes.** Run it again after cloning an old page or adding a new shell that has its own copy of the navbar.
- `add_browser_extensions_infobar.py` -- the **horizontal info bar** (Math Tutorials | Calculators | Tools | Mobile Apps | Puzzles | Memes ...) gets `Extensions` (-> `/browser`) between Mobile Apps and Puzzles on the ~499 pages that carry that row (2026-10-12), plus the root templates and 17 `build.mjs`. It is tagged `info-bar__item--utility`, so `info-bar-fit.js` hides it first and shows it only when it fits on one row (it precedes Subscribe / Store / Donate / Contact in the markup, so it has priority over them); verified: all shown at 1554px, hidden at 760px. `main.js` now skips any `/browser` link when it counts for the `:eq(N)` active-tab highlight (info bar AND the burger navbar), so no page's `info_bar_tab` / `current_tab` needed changing -- this also fixed the burger-menu highlight that the 2026-10-11 navbar link had shifted by one. Idempotent; **dry-runs by default (`-v` lists), `--apply` writes.**
- **Simulation box controls** (2026-10-12, `mes.fm/main_js/stage-ctl.js`): a "View" bar above every simulator box (`.fl-wrap`, `.lm-wrap`, `.bh-wrap`, `.mb-wrap`, `.pe-wrap`, `.ss-wrap`, `.ac-wrap`, `.mm-wrap`, `.ftl-wrap`) with **Small / Medium / Large**, a **corner grip** (drag any width, double-click = standard) and **Fill window** (the box covers the browser window, not the screen; Esc / Close leaves; the browser's own full screen stays on the existing button). Added by `build_tool_apps.py` (`STAGE_CTL` set) as a deferred script on those pages (fluid, light in a medium, black hole, mandelbrot, photoelectric, solar system, atomic clock + fountain clock, Michelson-Morley, FTL shadow); the 3D Fluid and the graphing calculators keep their own Window modes. The sims already lay out from their box's width, so a `max-width` on the box (plus a window `resize` event) is all it takes; **Medium (900 px, default on screens >= 1250 px) and Small (640 px) also cap the stage height (600 / 380 px) by the stage's own aspect, so a square scenario is not a giant square** (the aspect comes from the stage's inline px width / height, because a box caught mid-resize reports a wrong one). Fill sets the box's width from the window height x that aspect and the sims' own `full` checks (fluid, light in a medium, black hole, mandelbrot) also accept the `.mes-fill` class. Choice remembered per page (`mes-stage:<path>`). A new simulator page: give its box the `<prefix>-wrap` class, add the prefix to `PREFIX` in stage-ctl.js and its slug to `STAGE_CTL`.
- **Dark Mode 1.1.0 / Read Aloud 1.5.x** (2026-10-12): the Dark Mode toolbar button switches the site dark / light with one click (option `toolbar`: 'toggle' default or 'menu'; no popup is attached in toggle mode, right-click menu "Open the Dark Mode panel for this site" opens it in a small window with `?tab=<id>`); Read Aloud has a half-moon dark / light page button in the player (per-site, inverts the page with a stylesheet; **the player must flip itself back with a filter on its own fixed element, never on its host: a filter on an ancestor makes it the containing block of the fixed player and pushes it off screen** (the 1.5.0 bug fixed in 1.5.1)).
- **Extensions tab on the browser-extension pages** (2026-10-12): `build_tool_apps.py`'s new `tools_extra` option (`EXT_TAB`) puts an "Extensions" (-> /browser) tab right after Tools in the info bar of `read-aloud`, `videospeed`, `dark-mode`, `volume-booster`, `text-size` and the `browser` hub (always visible, not a hide-able utility; these pages' burger menu already had Browser Extensions from `add_browser_extensions_nav.py`). `main.js` ignores /browser links when counting active tabs, so nothing shifts.
- `add_percentage_app_link.py` -- an **iPhone App** link (a phone glyph (inline SVG; the page is already the Percentage Calculator so its own logo was redundant) + text -> the App Store listing `id6809222891`) in the nav of all 140 `mes.fm/percentagecalculator` pages (2026-10-12): a hide-able info-bar item right after Tools (utility class, so `info-bar-fit.js` shows it only when the row has room; at 1100px it is usually hidden because the bar is full) and a burger-menu item right BEFORE "Browser Extensions". Both carry `pc-applink`, which `percentagecalculator-app/build-www.py` strips from the app build. On a phone the calculator's Labels / Save / Clear all / Custom layout toolbar now wraps onto a second line (`.pc-toolbar` `flex-wrap`, links `nowrap`) instead of running off the left edge. The big "Percentage Calculator is now an app!" notification (`#android-testers-banner`) is separate and is meant to be removed a few weeks after the Android app is public; these nav links stay. When Android is public add a Google Play entry beside the iPhone one. **Menu-order rule (user, 2026-10-12): in the burger / side menu any Mobile Apps / app link ranks ABOVE "Browser Extensions"** -- so if a `Mobile Apps` (-> /mobile-apps) item is restored in the shared navbar (the `MOBILE-APPS-DROPDOWN-REMOVED` marker sits right after Browser Extensions today), put it before the Browser Extensions item (and teach `add_browser_extensions_nav.py` to insert after it). Idempotent; dry-runs by default (`-v` lists), `--apply` writes.

Release steps unchanged: `python3 build-www.py` (runs on ANY argument), `npx cap sync`, iOS: `xcodebuild archive -project ios/App/App.xcodeproj -scheme App -configuration Release -destination 'generic/platform=iOS' -archivePath ios/build/App.xcarchive -skipMacroValidation` then `xcodebuild -exportArchive ... -exportOptionsPlist` (method app-store-connect, team BS5YQPDA5X, manual, profile `Percent Calc App Store`) -> `ios/build/export/App.ipa`, uploaded by hand with Transporter; Android: JDK 21 `./gradlew bundleRelease` -> `android/app/build/outputs/bundle/release/app-release.aab`, uploaded in the Play Console.

- **BMR Calculator** (2026-10-14, `mes.fm/bmr-calculator`, green `#2b8a3e`; card in `calculators.html` "Everyday Math & Health"). Built by `build_tool_apps.py` from `tool_apps_src/bmr-calculator/` (`lib.js` = `BR`, tests `node tool_apps_src/bmr-calculator-tests.js`, 37 checks): Mifflin-St Jeor (default), revised Harris-Benedict, Katch-McArdle and Cunningham (the last two need body-fat %, else it falls back to Mifflin with a note), kg/cm mirrored with lb/ft/in, five activity multipliers (TDEE), goal table at -1 / -0.5 / -0.25 / 0 / +0.25 / +0.5 kg a week (7,700 kcal per kg) with a red flag under BMR or the 1,200 (women) / 1,500 (men) floor, goal-weight timeline, equation comparison, kcal + kJ. Share links are plain query strings (`?kg=&cm=&age=&sex=&act=&bf=&f=`) and `track.js` drops them; state in `mes-bmr-calculator:v1`. Registered like `bmicalculator` (TOOLS, tools sidebar collection, bottom ad, cross links, search index, hub card, `build_aside_recs`). **Visuals (same day):** energy-split bar (resting / digesting / movement) and organ-share bar (Elia 1992), a **"What if I eat...?" slider** with a week-by-week weight projection chart (`BR.project`: maintenance recomputed as weight changes, lean mass held fixed for the lean-mass equations; hover readout; goal line + week reached when a goal weight is set; `&kcal=` in share links), inline bars in the equation / activity tables, and a BMR-by-age chart. Art is Grok (icon cut from a checkerboard-baked JPG: green square found by colour, re-rounded; `img/bmr-calculator-icon.jpg` is the 900x600 tile, unused until a hub tile exists).
- `register_calc_tool.py` -- registers a new standalone calculator in every list `bmicalculator` / `bmr-calculator` is in (add_tool_page_controls TOOLS, add_sidebar tools collection, add_bottom_ad, add_cross_links + build_search_index CALC_DIRS, organize_hub_cards "everyday", build_aside_recs, a calculators.html card, `--private` also adds it to `track.js`'s query-dropping regex). Does not write the `APPS` entry or source folder. Dry-runs by default, `--apply` writes. Then: `build_tool_apps.py --only=<slug> --apply`, the four follow-ups, `git checkout` the churn on other pages (keep `calculators.html`, `newest.json`, `main_js/track.js`), `build_sitemap.py`, `build_search_index.py`, `build_aside_recs.py --apply`, `build_site_index.py --apply`.

UI: metric / US toggle (stored in kg / cm), gauge with category bands + goal pin, fat / lean bar, goal slider, method comparison, waist-to-height ratio, "Use it in the BMR calculator" link (`/bmr-calculator?...&bf=&f=katch`). Logos are PIL placeholders until Grok art lands.
- **Macro Calculator** (2026-10-14, `mes.fm/macro-calculator`, green `#1f6a2f`; calculators "Everyday Math & Health"): `lib.js` = `MC` on top of the BMR calculator's lib (`lib_from=["bmr-calculator"]`, so `BR` is a global; tests `node tool_apps_src/macro-calculator-tests.js`, 34 checks). Mifflin-St Jeor TDEE -> goal calories (7,700 kcal/kg) -> grams by preset split (balanced, high protein, low carb, keto, low fat, endurance) or custom protein / fat sliders (carbs fill the rest), donut, per-meal table, split comparison, protein g/kg check (1.2-2.0, or 1.6-2.4 while cutting), fibre 14 g/1,000 kcal. - **Ideal Weight / Water Intake / TDEE** (2026-10-14; calculators "Everyday Math & Health", each with Grok-art placeholders until real art lands):
  `ideal-weight-calculator` (`lib.js` = `IW`, tests `ideal-weight-calculator-tests.js`, 22 checks): Devine / Robinson / Miller / Hamwi (kg per inch over 5 ft) next to the healthy BMI band, range chart with your weight, formula table, ideal-weight-at-every-height chart with hover. `water-intake-calculator` (`lib.js` = `WI`, 27 checks): 32 ml/kg + 12 ml per exercise minute + climate (0-1,100 ml) + pregnancy +300 / breastfeeding +700, +-15% range, split bar, hourly sip plan chart, **daily tracker** (ring, +250/500/750 ml or 8/16/24 fl oz, custom, undo, streak, 14-day chart; localStorage `mes-water-intake-calculator:v1` `.log` by date, never in links). `tdee-calculator` is the BMR calculator's script and page in a TDEE-first mode (`src="bmr-calculator"`, `content-tdee.html`, `data-mode="tdee"` on `#br`, shared `js/bmr-calculator.js`: **rebuild both slugs together, bump `js_v` on both**), own text so the two pages are not duplicates. - `import_grok_art.py` -- imports a tool's Grok art from `~/Downloads/<name>-icon.jpg / -share.jpg / -tile.jpg` (`name=slug` pairs): the icon is cropped to its coloured rounded shape, a portrait one is NOT squashed but widened with its edge colour (corners dropped), re-rounded to `logo.png` 512 + `img/<slug>-logo.png` 352; the share image is centre-cropped to `logo-big.png` 1200x630; the tile becomes `img/<slug>-icon.jpg` 900x600. Bump the `?v=` on the `calculators.html` card afterwards. Dry-runs by default, `--apply` writes.

- **Calories Burned / Sleep** (2026-10-14; calculators "Everyday Math & Health", placeholder logos until Grok art lands): `calories-burned-calculator` (`CB`, tests 197 checks): 85 activities with 2011-Compendium METs, kcal = MET x kg x h (total or net MET-1), search + category chips, an activity builder (minutes, times a week) with weekly kcal, fat equivalent, a **150-minute guideline bar** (vigorous counts double, light 0), a top-activities chart, "how long to burn X kcal". `sleep-calculator` (`SL`, 86 checks; sleep link params dropped by `track.js`): bedtimes for a wake time / wake times for a bedtime in 90-minute cycles (adjustable cycle + fall-asleep time, AASM / NSF hours by age group), clickable options with a typical-night hypnogram (deep sleep early, REM late), evening plan (caffeine -6 h, alcohol -4 h, meal -3 h, screens -1 h), nap wake times, weekly sleep-debt chart (localStorage `mes-sleep-calculator:v1`).

Sleep and pregnancy pages' query strings are dropped by `track.js` (pregnancy and BSA are in its list).
- **PDF Tools family** (2026-10-15; `mes.fm/merge-pdf`, `split-pdf`, `organize-pdf`, `jpg-to-pdf`, `pdf-to-jpg`, `compress-pdf`; red like the editor, the same MES PDF Tools brand (`brand slug="pdf"`, so favicon / og:image / header are the editor's; the six slugs have no art of their own and one "PDF Tools: Merge, Split, Convert" card in `tools.html` links `/merge-pdf` with its own icon `img/pdf-tools-logo.png` (PIL placeholder until Grok art: `import_grok_art.py pdf-tools --apply` then bump the card's `?v=`)): ONE script `tool_apps_src/pdf-tools/app.js` (+ `lib.js` = `PT`, `node tool_apps_src/pdf-tools-tests.js`, 163 checks: page-range parser ("1-3, 5, 8-", `last`, reversed, errors), chunking, describe, names, image page fitting for A4 / Letter / Legal / A5 / same-size with margins and orientation, compress presets, move) and one `content.html` template; `data-mode` on `#pt` (merge / split / organize / images / toimg / compress) picks the behaviour, `vars.SEO` = `seo/<mode>.html`; the shared script is written to `merge-pdf/js/merge-pdf.js` (`js_path`) and all six `APPS` entries use it with `lib_from=["image-converter"]` (for `MESImg.zipStore`). pdf.js 3.11.174 + pdf-lib 1.17.1 load from cdnjs on first use; nothing is uploaded. **Merge** (drag / arrows to order, per-file page range), **Split** (by range -> one PDF per range, every N pages, extract picked pages from a thumbnail grid into one PDF; ZIP of the pieces), **Organize** (thumbnail grid: drag reorder, rotate, delete / restore, duplicate, add blank, reset), **JPG to PDF** (JPG / PNG placed without re-compression, others via canvas; rotate per picture; page size / orientation / margin), **PDF to JPG** (JPG or PNG at 72 / 150 / 220 / 300 dpi, page range, ZIP), **Compress** (Light = pdf-lib rewrite with object streams; Medium 150 dpi / q .78 and Strong 100 dpi / q .62 = every page becomes a JPEG; says so honestly when the result is not smaller). Password-protected PDFs cannot be changed here (message points to the editor). Verified in the browser for all six modes (scripted files in, downloads captured, outputs re-read with pdf-lib). Keyword redirects (`/pdf-merge`, `/combine-pdf`, `/rotate-pdf`, `/images-to-pdf`, `/pdf-to-png`, `/shrink-pdf` ...) were only really added on 2026-10-15 (this note used to claim they existed).
- **PDF Editor** (2026-10-15, `mes.fm/pdf`, red `#d9363e`; `/pdf-editor`, `/edit-pdf`, `/sign-pdf`, `/pdf-sign`, `/pdf-signature`, `/online-pdf-editor`, `/pdfeditor`, `/fill-pdf`, `/pdf-annotator` 308 to it; card in `tools.html` "Photos & Images"; a normal page in `add_sidebar.py`'s **tools collection** (Standard | Wide | Theatre + "More like this" sidebar, Wide by default via the inline snippet; the six PDF Tools pages too), no `.outer-container` width override, **no automatic Full window** on open (it hid the sidebar and the switch; Full window stays one click / key F), and a ResizeObserver on `#pd-view` re-fits a "fit width" zoom when the width mode, sidebar or window mode changes (`refit()` in `app.js`, keeps `PD.fitMode`); always-dark workspace (every colour a `--pd-*` custom property, incl. `--pd-paper` / `--pd-ink`, so the derived TOOL-DARK block leaves it alone; text in the font cards also gets an inline colour). Source `tool_apps_src/pdf-editor/` (`src="pdf-editor"` in the APPS entry because the slug is `pdf`): `lib.js` = `PDM` (pure; `node tool_apps_src/pdf-editor-tests.js`, 135 checks: Viewport PDF <-> canvas maths for 0 / 90 / 180 / 270, word wrap, `winAnsiOk`, text-line grouping, hit tests, resize, stroke simplify / smooth, alpha bounds, white-to-alpha, History), `core.js` (model: `PD.doc = { srcs[], pages[] }`, pages are `{src, index, blank, W, H, x0, y0, rot0, rot, annots[]}`; **annotations are stored in PDF user space of the SOURCE page** so rotating a page rotates what is on it; pdf.js 3.11.174 + pdf-lib 1.17.1 load from cdnjs on first open; JSON-snapshot undo; the editor wraps text with the same function the export uses; page render queue + overlay canvas per page), `tools.js` (select / move / resize, Text, **Edit text** (groups pdf.js text items into lines, covers the line with a patch in the sampled paper colour and puts an editable box with a similar font / size over it), Draw, Highlight (box or freehand), Shapes, Cover (match paper / white / black), Sign, Stamps (check, cross, dot, date), Comment, Image; option bar bound to the tool or the selected annotation; page list with rotate / duplicate / delete / add blank / add pages from another PDF / drag reorder), `sign.js` (type in six Google handwriting fonts / draw / upload with white-background removal; saved in localStorage `mes-pdf-editor:sigs`, max 8), `export.js` (**vector export**: pages are copied with pdf-lib, annotations drawn as real text / paths / images in standard fonts (text with characters outside WinAnsi is rasterised, in the editor too), comments become **real PDF `/Text` sticky notes**; **Flatten pages** mode renders each page + annotations to a JPEG at 100 / 150 / 220 dpi so redactions are permanent, used automatically for password-protected files and when black cover boxes exist). Verified in the browser by script: open, text / pen / shape tools, edit text, note, stamp, rotation, typed signature, vector and flattened export re-read by pdf.js. **Fill form** (`form.js`, key R; 2026-10-15): pdf.js `getAnnotations()` gives each Widget's rectangle / type; real `<input>` / `<textarea>` / `<select>` / checkbox / radio elements sit over the page (unrotated pages only) and what is typed becomes an ordinary text or check-mark annotation with a `formKey`, hidden from the overlay canvas while the tool is active (`paintAll` `skipForm`), so undo, export and flatten work as for everything else; the original widgets stay in the exported PDF unfilled. Not done: true reflowing text edits, OCR, digital (certificate) signatures, autosave / restore. Logos are PIL placeholders until Grok art lands.
- `build_pdf_page.py` -- hosts a PDF on a page (2026-10-15): the standard branded shell (`hive_mirror_template.html`, MES.fm brand) with an inline `<object>` PDF viewer (+ "Open the PDF" fallback for phones), a **Download the PDF** button, an optional alternative-download link and intro / outro text. `PAGES` holds the definitions (`noindex: True` keeps a page out of the sitemap and the site search; the Columbia page is **indexable** (Google, sitemap, site search) but listed in `add_sidebar_math.UNLINKED`, so no other page's sidebar cards or the random card point at it until the user links it from a math tutorial: then delete its slug from `UNLINKED`); the PDF is copied from `~/Downloads/<pdf name>` into `mes.fm/<slug>/`. First use: `mes.fm/em-304-problem-1-judy-wood` ("Space Shuttle Columbia Disaster Test Problem by Dr. Judy Wood", Clemson EM 304, test given 2003-02-06, five days after Columbia was lost 2003-02-01; OneDrive alternative link). The hosted Columbia PDF's own metadata Title was "Microsoft Word - 0301_EM304-T1d" (Word's export), which Chrome's PDF viewer shows in its toolbar: it was rewritten with pdf-lib (`setTitle` "Space Shuttle Columbia Disaster Test Problem by Dr. Judy Wood", Subject, Keywords, `DisplayDocTitle`; pages and look unchanged, 2.28 MB -> 2.10 MB). **Check a hosted PDF's Title / Author metadata before publishing it.** **The PDF itself must be saved as `~/Downloads/space-shuttle-columbia-test-problem-judy-wood.pdf` and the script re-run with `--apply`** (the OneDrive link needs a sign-in, so it cannot be fetched from here). Dry-runs by default, `--apply` writes.
- **Privacy policy** (2026-10-15, `mes.fm/privacy-policy.html`): new "Pictures and files you use in our tools" (browser-only) and "Reverse Image Search: a temporary copy of your picture" sections (one-hour copy, random link, noindex, hashed-IP upload counter, switch-off option); last-updated date moved to Oct 15, 2026. Keep it in step if the hosting changes or another tool starts uploading anything.
- **Short links** (2026-10-15): `/photo` -> `/photo-editor`; `/pdf-editor`, `/edit-pdf`, `/sign-pdf`, `/pdf-sign`, `/pdf-signature`, `/online-pdf-editor`, `/pdfeditor`, `/fill-pdf`, `/pdf-annotator` -> `/pdf` (the PDF editor's own page).
- **Reverse Image Search: sites open already showing the picture** (2026-10-15): only SauceNAO / IQDB accept a file posted from another page (Google Lens 403, TinEye 405, ascii2d needs a CSRF token), so every other site used to open its home page and need a manual Ctrl+V. Now, for a picture *file* with **"Let the sites load my picture automatically"** on (default, saved in `mes-reverse-image-search:v1` `.host`), pressing Open (a) opens one blank window per ticked site inside the click (so pop-up blockers allow it), (b) uploads a resized JPEG (<= 1600 px, <= 650 KB, quality / size stepped down until it fits) once to **`mes.fm/api/image-temp.js`**, (c) sends each window to its engine's link-based URL with the hosted link (`https://mes.fm/ri/<id>.jpg`, vercel.json rewrite to `/api/image-temp?id=`). The function stores the base64 in Upstash with a **1-hour expiry** under a random 128-bit id, accepts only JPEG signatures <= 700,000 bytes, rate-limits 40 uploads per IP per hour, serves `image/jpeg` with `noindex`; tests `node tool_apps_src/image-temp-tests.js` (17, fake Redis). If the upload fails (or the switch is off) it falls back to the old clipboard / paste flow. The picture is no longer "never uploaded" (page text and Privacy bullet say so); `localhost` skips hosting (no API there, test on 127.0.0.1 with `fetch` and `window.open` stubbed). Needs a deploy; uses the same Upstash env vars as `api/share.js`.
- **Reverse Image Search paste bug** (2026-10-15): pasting or dropping a picture left the engine list hidden until the tab was switched, because `loadFile()` called `setMode('file')` (-> `refresh()`) before `drawBase()` created `work`; it now calls `refresh()` after `drawBase()`.
- **Health & everyday tools, 2026-10-14** (each page built by `build_tool_apps.py`; run the usual follow-ups `add_tool_page_controls` / `add_sidebar` / `add_bottom_ad` / `add_cross_links` / `organize_hub_cards` and `git checkout` the churn on other pages; their share links carry private input, so `main_js/track.js` drops all query params on them).
  - **Ovulation family** (one source `tool_apps_src/ovulation-calculator/`: `lib.js` = `MESCycle` (dates as whole-day numbers, 59 node tests: `node tool_apps_src/ovulation-calculator-tests.js`), `app.js`, `app.css`, `content.html` with `{{VIEWS}}` / `{{SEO}}`, `seo/<slug>.html`): nine pages from `data-views` -- `ovulation-calculator` (ov), `period-calculator` (per, with a local cycle log), `due-date-calculator` (due), `pregnancy-test-calculator` (dpo), `conception-date-calculator` (conc), `weeks-pregnant-calculator` (weeks; baby sizes in `SIZE` in lib.js are the usual approximate comparisons). All share one localStorage state `mes-cycle:v1` (dates never leave the browser). Ovulation = next period minus the luteal phase (default 14), fertile window = 5 days before ovulation + ovulation day, chance bars = Wilcox 1995, due date = LMP + 280 (+ cycle - 28), conception +266, IVF transfer 261 / 263 for day 5 / 3; every page can export `.ics` events. Icons are real Grok art for ovulation / period / due date; the other three are placeholders (`img/<slug>-logo.png`, `<slug>/img/logo.png`, `logo-big.png`).
  - **Baby Growth Percentile Calculator** (`baby-growth-percentile-calculator`, source `tool_apps_src/baby-growth/`: `lib.js` = `MESGrowth` (WHO LMS: z = ((x/M)^L - 1)/(L S), percentile via the normal cdf, tested against WHO's published SD lines, `node tool_apps_src/baby-growth-tests.js`), `data.js` generated by `python3 build_growth_data.py --apply` from the WHO 'expanded tables' workbooks (weight / length-height / head circumference for age, boys and girls, 0-5 years, one row per 7 days), `app.js`): percentiles, usual range, corrected age for preterm babies until 2 years, SVG chart with the 3rd / 15th / 50th / 85th / 97th lines, local growth log (`mes-growth:v1`) with a "moved N main lines" flag. Not included: birth weight by gestational age (Fenton / INTERGROWTH) and the CDC 2-20 charts.
  - **Baby Feeding Log** (`baby-feeding-log`, `lib.js` = `MESFeed`, tests `baby-feeding-log-tests.js`): left / right breast timers with a next-side hint (left then right within 20 minutes = one feed), bottles (ml / oz), pumping, one-tap diapers, today's totals, localStorage `mes-feedlog:v1`.
  - **Archive** (`mes.fm/archive`, `tool_apps_src/archive/`: `lib.js` = `MESArchive` engine table + `cleanUrl` (tracking-parameter stripping) + URL builder, tests `node tool_apps_src/archive-tests.js` (74 checks incl. the extension's settings helper)): one address -> Wayback Machine (save / newest / calendar), archive.today (domain variants ph / is / today / md / vn / li / fo), Ghostarchive, Megalodon, Arquivo.pt, RemovePaywall and **ArcHive** (the third-party Hive-blockchain tool at dhenz14.github.io/archive, which takes `?url=` and fills in + starts its own extraction; the user signs with Hive Keychain / HiveAuth there), custom engines (`%s` / `{u}` / `{raw}`), popup-blocked queue, and a Wayback availability check (`archive.org/wayback/available`, cross-site allowed). Templates verified by hand 2026-10-14 (archive.ph answers curl with 429 but works in a browser; Memento Time Travel and 12ft.io were dead and left out). `?u=` fills the box (used by the bookmarklet). **MES Archive extension** (`mes.fm/archive/extension/`, plain MV3, `build_archive_extension.py --apply` copies `lib.js` to `extension/engines.js`, makes the icons from `archive/img/logo.png` and zips `mes.fm/archive/mes-archive.zip`; bump `manifest.json` version for a release): toolbar popup with the ticked sites (storage.sync), Alt+Shift+A, right-click "Archive this page / link", options page for custom sites, only `storage` + `activeTab` + `contextMenus` permissions. The download / install section is on the `/archive` page itself (`#extension`) and has a tile on `/browser`. **Hive hand-off (extension 1.1.0, `extension/hive.js`):** ArcHive has no server and on a computer only accepts a page through its own bookmarklet (ARCHIVE_READY / ARCHIVE_EXTRACT postMessage with a session nonce); the extension does that step itself: `collectPage()` runs in the page (main world: Twitter tweet-only / replies, YouTube transcript, Wayback metadata, like the bookmarklet), ArcHive opens in a normal tab, and `sendToArchive()` posts the payload with ArcHive's own `_archiveSessionNonce` and `CURRENT_BOOKMARKLET_VERSION` read from its page, so ArcHive updates do not break it; falls back to `?url=` for links and unreadable pages. Needs `scripting` + host access to dhenz14.github.io. Verified against the live ArcHive (it answered 'Not Yet Archived / Content extracted and ready to archive'); never loaded as a real extension in the dev environment: the popup logic was exercised with a mocked `chrome` API.
  - **Contraction Timer** (`contraction-timer`, `lib.js` = `MESContr`, tests `contraction-timer-tests.js`): one big button, 5-1-1 / 4-1-1 / 3-1-1 / 5-1-2 rule detection on the trailing run of qualifying contractions, localStorage `mes-contractions:v1`, wake lock, CSV / copy.
  - **EXIF Viewer** (`exif-viewer`, `lib.js` = `MESExif` reads JPEG / PNG / WebP / TIFF EXIF + XMP + C2PA flags, tests `exif-viewer-tests.js`; HEIC is detected but not read): GPS warning with map links, serial-number flag, "download a copy without metadata" (canvas re-save).
  - **Reverse Image Search** (`reverse-image-search`, `lib.js` = engine table, tests `reverse-image-search-tests.js`): link mode opens every ticked engine already searching; file mode POSTs the file straight to SauceNAO / IQDB (verified) and copies the image to the clipboard for the others (Google Lens answers 403 and TinEye 405 to cross-site uploads); crop / rotate / flip; no face-recognition sites on purpose.
  - **Domain Lookup** (`domain-lookup`, `lib.js` = `MESDomain` findings, tests `domain-lookup-tests.js`, API `mes.fm/api/domain-lookup.js`): RDAP (IANA bootstrap, thin registries follow the registrar record), DNS over HTTPS (Cloudflare), RDAP for the IPs, reverse DNS; the Internet Archive first capture is a second call (`?only=wayback`) because it is slow and sometimes down. Only fixed services are fetched and the name is validated (no IPs / localhost), so it is not an open proxy; the site itself is never visited.

- **mes.fm/moon alignment pass** (2026-10-20, hand-edited `moon/index.html` + `moon/js/moon-row.js?v=3`): the My Row preview table (`.row-preview`) has centred headings and values (`text-align:center; vertical-align:middle`, headings still wrap at 8.5em, tinted header row). Default / Wide / Theater are one segmented switch (`.row-views` > `.row-views-in` > `.row-view-btn`, ids unchanged), right-aligned above the table; the copy buttons stay in `.row-actions`. **Wide / Theater placement is done in JS**, not with a CSS `left:50%` breakout: `place()` in moon-row.js sets width = 94% of `clientWidth` (max 1400 px, never narrower than the column) or the full `clientWidth`, plus a `margin-left` measured from `.container`'s real position, and re-runs on resize, a ResizeObserver on `<html>` and a MutationObserver on `<html>`'s class (toc-flip / jump-aside page modes move the column off centre: the old breakout landed about 285 px off screen in Theatre mode, and 100vw made the page scroll sideways). The widened box is opaque with z-index 10 (above the fixed Jump-to list and the z-9 rail, below the floating bar at z 15). Section headings stay left-aligned like the other tool pages; the fold arrow is `display:inline` after a ` ` so it never wraps alone. In dark mode the page's own scroll areas and form controls use `color-scheme: dark`. The theme classes on this page are `body.light` / `body.dark` (not `dark-mode`).
- **Photo Editor round 3** (2026-10-20, `tool_apps_src/photo-editor/`, js_v 22): (1) **Panels grip**: it was dead because the grip and the search-results box shared the class `.pe-sres` (the search CSS moved the grip to an 8 px square at the bottom of the scrolling panel column, and the phone rule `display:none` on it also hid search results on phones). It is now `.pe-sgrip`, its own flex item between picture and panels (CSS `order`: tools -4, tres -3, [sl: side -2, grip -1], stage 0, grip 1, side 2, [tr: tres 3, tools 4]). Pointer drag (mouse / touch / pen, `touch-action:none`), arrow keys when focused (Shift = 4 em, Home/End min/max, Enter or double-click reset), `role=separator` with px aria values, width `UI.sw` (em) clamped by `MESPixel.panelClamp` (12-48 em, picture keeps 240 px) plus CSS `max-width:max(12em, calc(100% - 22em))`. Never reuse a class name across widgets in app.css: check with grep first. (2) **Form fields**: every input / select / textarea in the editor is styled with `#pe ...` selectors (workspace colours, ring, caret, ::selection, `color-scheme:dark` on `.pe`), because inputs created without a `type` matched no rule (the rename box was white on white) and the derived TOOL-DARK GENERIC rule (0,2,1) beats class rules. Use the `#pe` prefix for any colour that must survive `body.dark-mode`. The layer rename box stops click / mousedown from reaching the row (the row click rebuilt the list and lost the half-typed name) and only commits when the name changes. (3) **Save project**: File menu items take `hi`, `icon`, `tag`, `title` (Save project = `hi`, first in the save group, "keeps layers" tag); top-bar `#pe-save` button with a dot while `PE.dirty` (`PE.saveState()`); menus are `width:max-content` up to 28em. `.pe button{font:inherit}` resets font-weight at (0,1,1), so bold rules need `.pe .x` or `#pe .x`. (4) **Files and folders** (`PE.dlgFolder(focus)` in files.js; File -> "Where are my images?" and "Connect a folder"): where each layer came from, the folder, the autosave. Provenance is `L.src` (`{how: open|drop|paste|folder|folder-ro|psd|project|autosave|merge|made, file, path, dir, bytes, w, h, t, hk, copy, ai[]}`, set by `PE.openImageSource(src, name, meta)` / `PE.placeSource(src, name, at, meta)` / openFiles `opts.how/dir/hk`, `PE.markAi(L, what)` in ai/erase/upscale). It is never changed in place (history snapshots share it), is kept in snapshots and `.mesproj` (without `hk`), and is described by `MESPixel.describeSource`. `PE.doc.file` = where the document came from. Folder file handles live only in memory (`PE.srcHandles[hk]`), so Move is offered only during the session in which the picture was added from the connected folder. Without `window.showDirectoryPicker` (Brave by default: brave://flags/#file-system-access-api; Safari, Firefox, phones) the dialog explains this and offers a read-only `<input webkitdirectory>` folder, and saving goes to Downloads. Move = confirm, then `FileSystemHandle.move()`; if that fails: copy, check the size, `removeEntry`; a name clash gets `name (2).ext` via `MESImg.uniqueName`. Saving into the folder asks before replacing. Clear autosave deletes the IndexedDB key `auto` (asks first). Custom properties must not contain colour keywords (`--pe-white` became `var(--pe-#333333)` in TOOL-DARK); they are now `--pe-hi` / `--pe-lo`.
- **Dark Mode 1.5.0** (2026-10-20, `mes.fm/dark-mode/extension/`): a click switches only that tab. The page keeps a timestamped sessionStorage record `mesdmTab` = `{m,h,t}`, and pure `MESDM.tabKeep` keeps it only for reload / back_forward / same-origin link (not for opened pages, typed / restored ones, or after 30 min away, `TAB_AWAY`). The service worker also keeps `{tabId: {h, m}}` in `chrome.storage.session` (background.js `withTabs`; pages send `hello` on every load, so leaving the site, closing or restarting drops it). Duplicated / restored / opened tabs have new ids, so `tabFinal` needs both to agree. `migrate()` (stored v<3) removes old per-site Dark / Light entries from the pre-1.3 one-click era (keeps Off), resets scope 'all' to 'tab' and the built-in default setup to manual, keeps other chosen setups, and leaves a `mesdmReset` note in storage.local for the options page. 'All pages' now flips dark <-> manual. Content scripts check `chrome.runtime.id` in every timer and observer and remove their style when the extension is reloaded. A new copy empties older `style#mesdm-style` elements and fires `mesdm-stop`. On install / update the service worker re-injects into all open tabs (needs `host_permissions <all_urls>`). The tooltip and popup show `MESDM.explain()` (why a page is dark). Tests: `node tool_apps_src/dark-mode-tests.js` (195). For a real-browser check, headless Brave accepts `--load-extension` with `--disable-features=DisableLoadExtensionCommandLineSwitch`, and `--enable-unsafe-extension-debugging` allows CDP `Extensions.loadUnpacked` (call it again on the same path to simulate an update). Without the `tabs` permission the service worker cannot see tab URLs.
- **Matrix Calculator** (2026-10-20, `mes.fm/matrix-calculator`, sky blue `#0369a1`; neutral name "MES Matrix Calculator", the machine-learning operations keep their own "Machine learning" group in the operation list; card in `calculators.html` "Algebra & Calculus"). Built by `build_tool_apps.py` from `tool_apps_src/matrix-calculator/` (`lib.js` = `MESMat`, `node tool_apps_src/matrix-calculator-tests.js`, 22 checks). **Every input is a grid** (`G[k].cells` = cell text for a / b / c, the source of truth; `S.a/b/c` hold the same matrix as compact text "1 2; 3 4" for localStorage `mes-matrix:v1` and share links `?a=&b=&c=&op=&p=&act=&cm=1`; old links and saves still load, and the query is cleared after loading). Cells take expressions through `MESMat.num` (a small recursive-descent evaluator, no eval: fractions, pi, e, ^, √, sqrt / sin / cos / tan / exp / ln / log10, 30°, 50%, implicit 2pi, LaTeX bits). Blank cells count as 0 (placeholder "0" plus a note). Bad cells are outlined red, with an error naming the row and column. Up to 50 × 50. **Paste parser** `MESMat.parseTable(text)` -> `{rows (cell text), format, notes}`: spreadsheet tabs (blank cells, thousands separators, decimal comma, a header row or label column skipped), plain text (`;` / new lines / `|`, spaces / commas), MATLAB `[1 2; 3 4]` (trailing `'` transposes), NumPy / Python `[[..]]`, `np.array(...)` / `torch.tensor(...)` / SymPy `Matrix(...)` (trailing `.T`), LaTeX bmatrix / pmatrix / array (`&`, `\\`, `\frac`, `\sqrt`, `\pi`), Mathematica `{{..}}` (`Sqrt[2]`, `Pi`), Markdown tables. Pasting into the first cell replaces the matrix; pasting elsewhere fills from that cell and grows the grid. Size changes, quick fills, paste and Use as A / B offer an Undo link under the grid. "Edit as: Grid | Grid + text | Text" (`S.view`) shows a text box kept in sync both ways: text is parsed 350 ms after typing stops, and unreadable text leaves the grid alone. Results: Copy as Text / Spreadsheet (TSV) / LaTeX / NumPy (`MESMat.matText`) and Use as A / B, with a picker when an operation gives several matrices. Clicking a number of A × B highlights its row of A and column of B in the input grids. The k / n / temperature values are kept separately in `S.pv`. **Eigenvalues of non-symmetric matrices** use `hqrEig` (EISPACK balance + Hessenberg + Francis double-shift QR, about 1 ms at 50 × 50; the old unshifted QR took seconds). `rref` keeps a copy of the matrix after each step only when it has at most 240 cells (augmented part included), and the page lists at most 150 steps. **Dark-mode gotcha:** a light rule written with the `border:` shorthand gets a TOOL-DARK twin that also uses the shorthand, which resets per-side widths (the bracket shapes became boxes). Write border width / style / colour long-hand for shapes like these, and prefix hand-written dark rules `body.dark-mode #mx.tu ...`. Rebuild: `build_tool_apps.py --only=matrix-calculator --apply`, then the usual follow-ups.
- **MES Mechanics of Materials** (2026-10-20, `mes.fm/materials` + `/youngs-modulus-calculator`, `/tensile-test-simulator`, `/shear-stress-calculator`, steel blue `#2f6690`; cards in calculators.html "Physics & Science"; ~47 keyword redirects in `vercel.json` such as `/youngs-modulus`, `/stress-strain-curve`, `/tensile-test`, `/shear-calculator`, `/torsion-calculator` (-> `/shear-stress-calculator?tab=torsion`), `/strength-of-materials` (-> `/materials`), `/poissons-ratio-calculator` (-> `/materials?tab=poisson`), `/thermal-stress-calculator`, `/materials-table`). ONE source `tool_apps_src/materials/` (`lib.js` = `MESMech`, `app.js`, `app.css`, `content.html` with `{{VIEWS}}` / `{{PAGE}}` / `{{SEO}}`, `seo/<slug>.html`) and one served script `materials/js/materials.js` (the three sub-pages use `src="materials"`, `js_path`, `brand=MAT_BRAND`, `tab=1..3`, nav from `MAT_NAV_INFO` / `MAT_NAV_MENU` at the top of build_tool_apps.py). `data-views` on `#mt` picks the tabs: axial (any 4 of F, A, L, ΔL, σ, ε, E solve the rest; newest typed value wins and older ones are worked out again (`axialSolve(givens newest-first)`); shapes back-solve their size; draggable force arrow; safety factor; Euler buckling on a push), tensile (Hollomon / Ramberg-Osgood fitted to E, 0.2% offset yield, UTS and elongation, necking at Considère, Lüders plateau + Swift law for mild steel, brittle, cold-drawing plastics, Gent rubber; `saneCurveMat` clamps your own material; unload/reload path, true curve, resilience/toughness areas, compare 3), shear, torsion, poisson (bar / 3D Hooke / elastic constants), thermal, table (30 materials, Ashby log-log chart). Material values are TYPICAL (sources on the table tab); edit `MATS` in lib.js and run `node tool_apps_src/materials-tests.js` (519 checks: textbook answers, every material's curve hits its own E / yield / UTS / elongation, Considère = n, 400 random custom materials stay finite). State: localStorage `mes-materials:v1` (shared by the four pages; tab remembered per page); share links are plain query strings (`?tab=axial&m=a36&u=si&F=10kN&d=10mm&L=1m&o=F,A,L,E&sh=round&dir=t`; field keys are the id without prefix, `3sx` / `cE` on the Poisson tab). Gotchas: the shell's reset paints every div/span/p #333, so app.css has `:where(#mt) :is(div, span, ...) {color:inherit}` and all colours are `--mt-*` custom properties restated under `body.dark-mode #mt.mt`; `.tu-field {display:flex}` beats the `hidden` attribute, hence `#mt [hidden] {display:none !important}`; labels are not uppercased because CSS uppercase turns σ/ν/ε and d/D into wrong symbols. Logos are PIL placeholders until Grok art lands (`materials/img/logo.png`, `logo-big.png`, `img/<slug>-logo.png`). Rebuild: `build_tool_apps.py --only=materials,youngs-modulus-calculator,tensile-test-simulator,shear-stress-calculator --apply`, bump `js_v` on all four when the script changes, then the usual follow-ups.
- **Video Compressor: constant quality** (2026-10-20, `tool_apps_src/compress-video/`, js_v 2). "Choose a quality" = an H.264 quantizer for every frame (WebCodecs `bitrateMode: "quantizer"` + `encode(frame, {avc: {quantizer}})`), like HandBrake's RF. Presets in `lib.js` `PRESETS`: High 21, Balanced 23.5 (`qStepper` alternates 23/24), Small 27, Smallest 30; a key frame every 10 s (`keyEvery`). On adding a file, `runProbe()` in `app.js` encodes the `probeWindows()` stretches (each from a source key frame, about 300 frames, picked in `mp4Info` from the mp4box sample table) at q 24 and, for two windows, at 30. That gives `{bitsP, bitsK, sP, sK}` for `cqVideoBps` / `qForBps` (bisection) and checks that the encoder obeys the quantizer (`probeSlope` null means fall back). Results are cached per `WxH@fps` in `f.qps`; probes run one at a time (`qChain`) and never while compressing (`kick()`). `chooseQ` caps the result at 85% of the source bitrate ("capped" note). Target-size mode uses the same model (3% margin, never better than High: "roomy" note), and `retuneQ` makes one corrected pass when the result is more than 3% over. WebM (VP9 takes no quantizer here) and the software fallback get `VBR_FACTOR` x the constant-quality bitrate. Measured on a 79 s 1080p talk-show clip against x264 fast RF 22 (9.34 MB, VMAF 96.03): Balanced 11.52 MB (video 1.30x) at VMAF 96.18; the old fixed-bitrate Balanced made 39.5 MB. VP9/AV1 software and hardware HEVC were NOT more efficient than hardware H.264; contentHint had no effect. **Gotchas:** mp4-muxer snaps timestamps to `frameRate`, so pass it only via `V.muxFrameRate()` (true constant whole-number rate; `cfr` comes from the sample durations), otherwise VFR / 29.97 fps sources get duplicate timestamps. The encode loops wait on the codecs' `dequeue` events (`signal()`), not setTimeout: background tabs throttle timers (the old code took 23 s instead of 1.8 s in a hidden tab). 10-bit sources (`needs8bit`) go through a canvas but keep the hardware encoder. Brave hides `showSaveFilePicker`: the caps line shows an amber chip and a brave://flags/#file-system-access-api hint. `?debug=1` exposes `window.MESVidDebug` (files, plans, probe results) for testing. Tests: `node tool_apps_src/compress-video-tests.js` (142).
- **Local preview gotcha** (2026-10-20): `preview_start {name:"mes-fm"}` fails / serves 404s because macOS blocks that process from ~/Documents; run `python3 -m http.server 8430 --bind 127.0.0.1` in `mes.fm/` from Bash in the background and open it with the Browser pane instead.

- **Round 2026-10-23** (one session, parallel builders):
  - **Type it in words** (`mes.fm/main_js/mes-ask.js`, `MESAsk`, no AI service; tests `node tool_apps_src/mes-ask-tests.js`): `calc()` (English arithmetic -> calculator expression), `percent()` (-> one of the ten Percentage Calculator rows: eq 1-5, 7-10), `graph(text, 2|3)` (shapes / equations -> grapher rows + box + slider meta: sphere, cube, cylinder, cone, torus, ellipsoid, helix, plane, spherical harmonic; circle, ellipse, line through two points, parabola, sine wave), `cas()` (units / diff / integrate / solve / factor / limit). Loaded via `pre_js` on calculator, 2D / 3D graphing, CAS; the Percentage Calculator has a hand-made `PC-ASK` block (hidden when the script is missing, e.g. the app build). Grapher rows: a row that fails to parse but reads as words shows "Press Enter to graph: ...".
  - **Calculator SI prefixes** (`tool_apps_src/calculator/lib.js` `PREFIX` / `PREFIX_RE` / `engFmt`): a prefix letter straight after a number (4.7k, 2M, 5m, 10u / 10µ, 3n, 22p) unless more letters follow (2pi, 3sin keep working); optional unit label after it (4.7kΩ, 100nF, 5ms) is ignored and reported. `#mc-note` line under the answer: "Read as ...", the engineering form, prefix help. "SI prefixes" chip = always show the engineering form.
  - **CAS units** (`engine.py` `op_units`, VERSION 3; `cas-client.js` V 3): Units tab; `5 km to mi`, `5 kg * 9.81 m/s^2` (-> 49.05 N, best named derived unit), temperatures as offsets, dimension mismatch explained. Unit names come from our own `_unit_table()` whitelist (sympy.physics.units quantities); `9.58 s` is grouped as `(9.58*s)` before parsing so `100 m / 9.58 s` divides by the whole amount. Tests appended to `cas-engine-tests.py` (units: 15).
  - **3D grapher spherical harmonics**: `ylm(l, m, θ, φ)` (real, orthonormal, Condon-Shortley) and `legendre(l, m, x)` in `G3.makeM()`; examples can carry slider meta (`[name, rows, box, {l: [min, max, step]}]`, saved as a 4th element of `s.p`); `?ex=<part of an example name>` opens an example (the spherical-harmonics page's sidebar CTX links use it).
  - **BMI chart** (all 12 languages): the weight-for-height box is `#bm-wchart` (it was a second `#bm-chart`, so the history chart overwrote it); "Try other numbers on the chart" drags the point with the range frozen (`chartSvg` / `chartPoint` take `o.range`), Use these numbers / Back to my numbers (i18n keys `chartTry`, `chartTryHint`, `chartUse`, `chartBack`).
  - **Speed Reader saved texts** (`mes-speedreader:lib`, each remembers its word position); the Read aloud grip also works in full screen (`.sr-pane.is-sized`). **Timer Display**: a Sound on / off button in the bar (`S.soundPrev` restores the chosen sound); no mute icon on stopwatch tiles. **Search Engines**: Clear "Also" extras button.
  - **New pages** (each with node tests `tool_apps_src/<slug>-tests.js`, placeholder PIL logos until Grok art, registered with a one-off script = the same edits as `register_calc_tool.py` for tools): `seo` (SEO Checker; `api/seo.js` + `api/_seo-lib.js`, which must stay byte-identical to `tool_apps_src/seo/lib.js`: `cp` it after editing, the tests check), `random-number-generator` + `dice-roller` (one source `tool_apps_src/random/`, `data-mode`), `statistics-calculator`, `backgammon` (engine in a Web Worker from the same script; `?noworker=1` for headless tests), `poker`, `birthday-personality` (original text, NOT from The Secret Language of Birthdays; `BP` is in `lib_from` of `birthday` and `zodiac`, which show a compact card; zodiac `LNY_FIX` corrects the Lunar New Year rule for 1916, 1920, 1985). tools.html got a **Games &amp; Chance** category (`organize_hub_cards.py` key `games`).
  - **`build_links_page.py`** writes `mes.fm/links/index.html` (MES Links in the hub shell: hand-kept SECTIONS + every card of tools.html / calculators.html, short links resolved through vercel.json, inline search + chips). Re-run it after adding tools; hand edits to the page are lost on a re-run.

### `build.mjs` pages: never rebuild without diffing first

Several pages (currently `911`, `911-alchemy`, `conspiracy`, `crypto`, `cubic-formula`, `djw`, `ferrocell-specular-reflection`,
`hutchison`, `hutchison-tom-sky`, `livestreams`, `math`, `mathiew`, `norman-patricia-ai-email`, `science`, `vector-functions-problems-plus`, all
under `mes.fm/`) have their own `build.mjs` (`npm run build`, usually fetching a Hive post) that regenerates
`index.html` from scratch. The repo-wide scripts above (`add_lightbox_zoom.py`, `add_image_lightbox.py`, and several
`optimize_pagespeed.py` transforms — the deferred-AdSense loader, the WCAG brand-blue darkening, and the `<img>`
lazy-loading pass) patch the *generated* `index.html` directly; they don't know `build.mjs` exists, so those patches
never land back in the template. **Running `npm run build` regenerates index.html purely from build.mjs and
silently reverts any patch that only ever lived in the generated HTML** — this has already happened for real more
than once (e.g. commit `cb410eb1` had to re-apply a dropped PageSpeed pass to `mes.fm/911`, and `mes.fm/911`'s
lightbox-zoom feature was silently lost the day after it shipped, by the very next Hive-mirror rebuild, and stayed
lost for a week before anyone noticed).

As of this writing, `911-alchemy`, `djw`, `ferrocell-specular-reflection`, `hutchison-tom-sky`,
`norman-patricia-ai-email`, and `vector-functions-problems-plus` all have `addImageLazyLoading()` back-ported into
`build.mjs`, applied to the whole generated page right before `writeFileSync` — it's a line-for-line JS port of
`optimize_pagespeed.py`'s `transform_images` lazy-loading rule (skip the first non-`data:` image on the page so LCP
isn't hurt, lazy-load every other `<img>` whose `src` is an `http(s)` URL not on `mes.fm`), verified by round-
tripping each page's committed `index.html` (strip `loading="lazy"` back out, rerun the new function, diff against
the original — all reproduce exactly). `ferrocell-specular-reflection`, `hutchison-tom-sky`,
`norman-patricia-ai-email`, and `vector-functions-problems-plus` additionally have the lightbox-zoom CSS/JS and the
darkened brand-blue back-ported (each carries a `NOTE:` comment right above its `return \`<!DOCTYPE html>`
explaining this — keep it in sync if you change a source script's template). `911-alchemy` has **no lightbox markup
at all** in `build.mjs` (added straight to `index.html` by `add_image_lightbox.py`/`add_lightbox_zoom.py`); `djw`
has neither the lightbox nor a color scheme that needs the contrast fix. Both carry a `WARNING:` comment about the
still-missing lightbox. `conspiracy`, `math`, `mathiew`, and `science` were authored recently enough that their
`build.mjs` was already in sync with the lightbox/AdSense/contrast patches as of this writing. (`math` did turn out to
be missing the lazy-loading pass -- its List View thumbnails lost `loading="lazy"` on a rebuild -- so it has
`addImageLazyLoading()` back-ported too, and its AdSense block matches the committed `index.html` byte for byte, so
`npm run build` in `mes.fm/math` now reproduces the committed page exactly, apart from any new entries.)

Note `mes.fm/math/build.mjs` emits **six** pages, not one: the tile hub `mes.fm/math/index.html` plus
`mes.fm/{math-qa,sequences-series,spherical-harmonics,vectors,vector-functions}/index.html` (one section each, from
its `PAGES` array) — never hand-edit those generated files, and `git status` after a build will show all six. (The
`cubic-formula` tile is also in `PAGES`, but flagged `ownBuild`: that page is generated by its own build, below.)
`mes.fm/math-qa` also has the search box + category chips of `/livestreams` (2026-09-26): `QA_CATEGORIES` in `math/build.mjs` (title regexes:
Aether -- Ionel Dinu's series, "What is the Aether?", Michelson-Morley, stellar aberration; Electromagnetism; Free Energy; Moon; 9/11), items
get `data-cats`, and the CSS/JS (`FILTER_CSS`/`FILTER_JS`, a copy of the widget in `livestreams/build.mjs` -- keep the two in step) are emitted
only for a section that sets `filter`, so the other five pages stay byte-identical. **Stats tab (2026-10-07):** each entry of the Q/A `extraViews` "Stats" list (`math-qa-72-stats`, `math-qa-71-stats`, newest first) inherits the `cats` of the Q/A with the same number (the build throws if none matches), and `wireFilter` now includes the Stats pane, so the search box and chips filter that tab like `/livestreams` does (the filter bar no longer hides there). New stats page: mirror it (clone the last `math-qa-N-stats` page), add it to `extraViews` in `math/build.mjs`, seed its `math/link-meta.json` entry by hand, rebuild and `git checkout` the rest of `link-meta.json`. After a `math` build, `git checkout math/link-meta.json`.

`mes.fm/hutchison/build.mjs` (2026-09-26) is **no longer a Hive mirror**: it was re-cloned from `math/build.mjs`, so `mes.fm/hutchison`
