via `add_bg_links.py`, which reads the Posts list; re-run it after adding a post). Tile art = the newest item of each section: `img/bg-videos-icon.jpg` (3:2 crop of the newest video's maxres thumbnail), `img/bg-posts-icon.jpg` (the Telegram screenshot fitted whole onto a matching dark 900x600 tile, since a 3:2 crop cut the message off),
`img/bg-logo.jpg` / `bg-logo-big.jpg` (512 square favicon / header logo and 1200x630 og:image = crops of the "Buzzword Bobby's Bowl of Babble" word-salad image, **for every bg page**: hub, `bg-videos`, `bg-posts` (their og:image is the big logo, not their tile icon) and `bg-notes`); replace those files (same names) for real art. `bg-notes` was converted to the standard branded mirror shell (`convert_mirror_pages.py`: `BRANDS['/bg']`, `PAGE_BRANDS['bg-notes']` with its own "Part of Bob Greenyer · Posts · Videos" box, a `BRAND_TABS` entry `BG | Posts | Videos`, and favicon + og:image from the bg logo; the converter now handles `.jpg` brand art). The **old `mes.fm/bg` page** ("BG & NS Links": Bob Greenyer's claims about Dr. Judy Wood and 9/11) moved to `mes.fm/bg-notes`; every link to it (911-posts, 911 `sections.mjs` + `link-meta.json`,
the Amaterasu mirror, sitemap) was repointed, and the hub shows it as a tile "BG & NS Links" (`img/bg-notes-icon.jpg` = 3:2 crop of the "Buzzword Bobby's Bowl of Babble" word-salad image from the article itself), next to a tile-only "MH370 Teleportation Psyop" tile (`img/mh370-icon.jpg`) like `/conspiracy`'s. Registered like mh370: `improve_meta_descriptions.GENERATED`, `add_hub_theatre.GENERATORS`.

`mes.fm/science/build.mjs` (2026-10-01) was converted the same way (cloned from `conspiracy/build.mjs`): tile hub + `mes.fm/{science-posts,science-videos}/index.html`
from `science/sections.mjs`, tile art `img/science-{posts,videos}-icon.jpg`. It now uses the shared blue family look, not its old amber accent. Its Videos are external
YouTube playlists (scraped for their thumbnail). The info-bar of the conspiracy and science families shows `Conspiracy|Science | Posts | Videos` (hub builds, and
`convert_mirror_pages.py`'s `BRAND_TABS` extras for the mirrors); the mirrors' "Part of" box links the matching section page via `add_conspiracy_section_links.py`
(both hubs; idempotent, `--apply` writes -- re-run after adding a mirror to a section list).

`mes.fm/livestreams/build.mjs` (2026-09-26) is the `math-qa` idea for *all* MES livestreams: one page, Grid View / List View of every
numbered stream (newest first) plus two more tabs -- **Stats** (the stats-screen pages, now `mes.fm/livestream-140-stats`, mirrored from the
Hive/Telegram post; add new ones to `STATS`) and **Trailers** (card grid). Cloned from `hutchison/build.mjs`. The list comes from
`livestreams/playlist.json`, a snapshot of the YouTube livestreams playlist written by `python3 update_livestreams_playlist.py --apply`
(needs yt-dlp; also picks each video's best existing thumbnail, maxres else mqdefault, caching earlier lookups) -- after a new stream/trailer:
run that, then `npm run build` in `mes.fm/livestreams` and diff. `classify()` in build.mjs sorts playlist entries into livestream
(`MES Livestream N`, plus `EXTRA_LIVESTREAM_IDS`), trailer (title has "trailer", or "UPCOMING LIVESTREAM") or skipped (short clips and the
"BLANK" placeholder -- each build prints what it left out). Cards go to YouTube (new tab) unless the video has a mes.fm mirror in `MIRRORS`
(livestream 140 and its two trailers, livestream 66's trailer); those mirrors' "Part of" boxes link back to `/livestreams`. The
unnumbered "INTERVIEW: All Things 9/11 with TLBNAWKI" is on the main tab by id. Stats-page thumbnails/links are scraped from the live page,
so a *new* stats page must be seeded by hand in `livestreams/link-meta.json` (image + Hive/Telegram `namedLinks`) until it is deployed.
Above the tabs is a search box + category chips with counts (like `/calculators`): `CATEGORIES` in build.mjs (Hutchison Effect, 9/11 Truth and
BeneficenceTV are title regexes; "MES Truth" is a *channel* filter on `playlist.json`'s `channel` = `@mestruth`, i.e. videos uploaded to
youtube.com/@mestruth, the rest being on Math Easy Solutions). Items carry `data-cats`; the inline `wireFilter()` filters Grid, List and
Trailers together, recounts the chips for the showing tab, hides itself on Stats, honours `#<category>` and Esc. To add a chip add one entry.
Above the search box is one line of reference links (`standalone` on the section: "Playlist" and "Troubleshooting Notes" -> the hand-built
Hive mirror `mes.fm/troubleshooting`, built from `hive_mirror_template.html` with the MES Livestreams brand). `livestreams/extra-links.json` maps a stream number to extra platform links (shown after YouTube in List View). Titles are shortened to
"N: rest" like math-qa. No per-stream mirror pages are generated (unlike `build_math_qa_mirrors.py`).

`mes.fm/cubic-formula/build.mjs` (cloned from `vector-functions-problems-plus/build.mjs`, so it carries the same
lazy-loading / lightbox / AdSense / contrast back-ports) emits **nine** pages: `mes.fm/cubic-formula/index.html`
(mirror of Hive `@mes/dzekfnxh`, plus a Playlist Grid/List section) and the 8 supporting video pages
`mes.fm/{quadratic-formula-complete-square,quadratic-formula-pq-substitution,cubic-formula-step-1-pq-substitution,
cubic-formula-step-2-vieta-substitution,cubic-formula-step-3-first-solution-y,cube-root-unity,
cubic-formula-step-4-solutions-y-cube-root-unity,cubic-formula-step-5-solve-x}/index.html` (from its `CHILDREN`
array and `child-template.html`, which was cut from `problems-plus-4-curvature-parametric-integrals`). Each child
page mirrors its own Hive video post plus the matching "## Step N" section of the main article's written notes;
edit the notes in the Hive article and rebuild, don't hand-edit. The `youtube`/`telegram` ids in `CHILDREN` are
deliberate overrides: the Hive posts' link rows are wrong (Step 2's YouTube link is Completing-the-Square's, and
every Telegram link is the previous video's). Child pages play 3Speak via hls.js and swap themselves for the
YouTube embed if playback fails. The main page's Jump-to menu also lists the seven `## Step N` headings (nested
under "Derivation of Cubic Formula"), and Jump-to links expand a collapsed chapter before scrolling. `git status`
after a build shows the 9 pages plus `playlist-meta.json`.

Before running `npm run build` on any of these pages, `git diff --stat` (or a full diff) the result against the
previously committed `index.html` and confirm you're not losing lines you don't recognize — don't assume success.
If a rebuild does drop a patch that isn't back-ported into that page's `build.mjs`, re-run the matching repo-wide
script afterward (e.g. `python3 add_lightbox_zoom.py`) rather than hand-editing the generated HTML.

## Site structure conventions

Within a given subdomain directory:
- `index.html` is the homepage; other `*.html` files are individual content/tool pages.
- `js/` holds that site's calculator/interactive logic (vanilla JS, no framework).
- `img/` holds that site's images.
- `contact.html`, `privacy-policy.html` are boilerplate pages duplicated with site-specific branding across nearly
  every subdomain — when fixing one (e.g. a broken asset link), check whether the same issue exists in the other
  subdomains' copies.
- `mes.fm/main_js/` and `mes.fm/main_img/` hold shared assets for the main `mes.fm` site itself (nav, ads loader,
  disqus loader, lazysizes) — distinct from the per-subdomain `js/`/`img/` folders.
- `mes.fm/links/index.html` (a link directory page, "MES Links") is edited frequently and directly for small link
  additions/updates — most recent commit history is exactly this ("Add Vector Functions Review link" and its
  follow-up revisions).

When fixing something broken on one page, check whether the same broken pattern (stale asset path, malformed
URL, missing responsive fix) recurs across the other subdomain directories before considering the task done —
the repair scripts above exist because these issues are systemic (copy-pasted boilerplate across ~12 near-
identical sites), not isolated to one file.

- **MES Favourites** (2026-10-15, `mes.fm/fav`, amber `#a15c07`; card in `tools.html` "Media & Web"; wide + ad-free like the Site Index) -- a personal start page: search the whole site (the same `MESSearch` index as `/search`, loaded via `pre_js`), press Add to put a page in a group, and arrange tiles by drag (pointer events with FLIP animation and a ghost; **Alt while dropping copies**; Escape cancels; mouse drags anywhere on a tile, touch only from the dots; arrow keys on the dots move tiles / groups for keyboard users; dropping on a collapsed group opens it after 0.5 s; auto-scroll near the window edge), copy-to-another-group menu, remove / delete with Undo toast, rename, fold (Collapse / Expand all), and the search box also filters the favourites. Built by `build_tool_apps.py` from `tool_apps_src/fav/` (`content.html`, `app.js`, `app.css`, no lib). State = localStorage `mes-fav:v1` (`{groups:[{id,name,open,items:[path]}], to}`), cross-tab via the `storage` event. **Moving between devices:** `/fav?s=1<base64url JSON [[group name,[paths]],...]>` (no server; titles / thumbnails are resolved from the search index on load); opening a link on a browser that already has favourites offers Replace / Merge / Cancel (Undo toast), plus a JSON backup file. `main_js/track.js` drops query params on `/fav`. Registered in `add_tool_page_controls` TOOLS, `add_cross_links` / `build_search_index` TOOL_DIRS and `organize_hub_cards` (media); deliberately NOT in `add_sidebar` / `add_bottom_ad`. Logos are PIL placeholders (`fav/img/logo.png` 512, `logo-big.png` 1200x630, `img/fav-logo.png` 352) until Grok art lands. **Star button + menu link (2026-10-15, `main_js/site-search.js` `favButton()` / `favMenu()`, so on every page that has the header controls):** a star left of the search button toggles the current page (`favPath()`: path without `index.html` / `.html` / trailing slash, the same form as the search index) in the user's `mes-fav:v1` list (added to the last-used group, toast with an "Open favourites" link; removing shows an Undo toast; filled amber when saved; synced across tabs via `storage`; hidden on `/fav` itself). It is an extra (`hc-x`) button, so on phones it hides behind the chevron with read aloud / text size. `favMenu()` inserts a "★ My Favourites" item before the Browser Extensions item of every burger / side menu; `main.js` skips `/fav` (like `/browser`) when counting the active tab. Grok art imported 2026-10-15 (`import_grok_art.py` now skips a missing `-tile.jpg`). **Short links (2026-10-15):** `mes.fm/api/fav.js` (tests `node tool_apps_src/fav-api-tests.js`, 25 checks, fake Redis; same Upstash vars as `api/share.js`) stores a list under a random 9-char id (`/f/:id` is a `vercel.json` rewrite to `/fav?f=:id`; `track.js` reports those as `/fav`): only group names (<= 60 chars, tags / control characters stripped) and site-relative paths (`^/(?!/)[A-Za-z0-9-_.~%/]{0,150}$`, no `..`) are accepted (<= 40 groups, 400 pages, 24 KB, 30 writes per IP per hour), a random edit key lives in the browser (`mes-fav:short`, only its SHA-256 is stored) and lets the page auto-update (1.5 s debounce) or delete the list; two-year TTL renewed on read. The long `?s=` link stays as a fallback, and **both import paths now reject `//host` and backslash paths** (`clean()` in `app.js`; a shared link could otherwise have made a tile open another site). Needs a deploy to work live (the local Python server has no `/api`). Custom (non-mes.fm) links are deliberately NOT supported (owner's call: the site must not become a link-shortener / open redirector that could get flagged). Ideas not built: group colours.
- **Shapes / Geometry / Trigonometry** (2026-10-18; calculators "Everyday Math & Health", purple / blue / orange placeholder logos until Grok art lands; keyword redirects such as `/volume`, `/angles`, `/trig`, `/unit-circle` in `vercel.json`; tests `node tool_apps_src/<slug>-tests.js`): `shapes-calculator` (`SH`, 187 checks) = volume, surface area, area and perimeter of 12 flat shapes and 15 solids with every formula, unit picker (mm to mi), litres / gallons / acres, "work it backwards" (scale every length to hit a target volume or area), SVG diagram per shape, `?s=&u=&<dims>` links. `geometry` (`GE`, 135 checks) = Geometry & Angle Lab, four tabs (`?tab=tri|lines|circle|poly`): draggable triangle (angles, sides, area, kind, circumcircle / incircle / Euler line, similar triangle by scale about A with ratios), two lines + transversal (eight angles, click one to colour corresponding / alternate / co-interior / vertical / linear-pair partners, parallel or tilted lower line), circle theorems (central vs inscribed, Thales, cyclic quadrilateral, tangent-chord), angle explorer (type, complement, supplement, conjugate, rad / grad) and regular / free polygon angle sums. `trigonometry` (`TR`, 299 checks) = unit circle (drag, six functions, exact values for multiples of 15 and 18 degrees, wave strip), calculator (degrees / radians / grad, "3pi/4" parsing, inverse functions with every solution in 0-360), triangle solver (right triangles, SSS, SAS, ASA / AAS, ambiguous SSA with two solutions), sinusoid graphs (A, B, C, D sliders, tan asymptotes), trig tables and an identity checker (18 identities). Registered with `register_calc_tool.py`; after editing run `build_tool_apps.py --only=shapes-calculator,geometry,trigonometry --apply` + the usual four follow-ups (`git checkout` the churn on other pages, keep `aside-random.json`, `newest.json`). **3D solids viewer (same day):** `SH.mesh(id, dims, n)` in `lib.js` builds a closed, outward-oriented triangle mesh for all 15 solids (surfaces of revolution + flat convex polyhedra), tested by comparing mesh volume and area with the formulas (232 checks); the shapes page draws it on a plain canvas (painter's algorithm, drag to rotate, wheel / pinch zoom, spin, Solid / See-through / Wireframe, orange dimension labels, uses example dims until valid numbers are typed). **Interactive figures (2026-10-18, user request):** the viewer now also draws the 12 flat shapes as a scale drawing (`flatMesh()` in `lib.js`, tested against the area formulas, 256 checks) and every dimension line ends in a white dot you drag to change that length (pointer projected onto the line's screen direction, value x t, applied through the input field so everything recalculates; the picture scale is frozen during a drag and the triangle ignores drags that break the triangle inequality); labels show `key = value unit` and can be dragged (offsets in localStorage `mes-shapes-calculator:v1:lab`, Reset view clears them). The Geometry lab has movable labels in every figure (ids `<svg id>:<n>`, offsets in its state `lab`), a Text size slider (`--gefs`) and a Figure zoom slider (viewBox, drag the background to pan, Reset labels). The Trigonometry unit-circle and calculator tabs have mirrored Degrees / Radians (/ Gradians) fields instead of a unit toggle. **Round 2 fixes (same day):** dot dragging is now measured from where the drag started (a clamp of 0.2x-5x and a learned gain, because some lines grow from one end and some from the middle) so it can no longer run away to a sliver; each dimension also has a slider under the picture (centred on the current value, one decade either way, the picture re-fits while sliding). Geometry's Text size / Figure zoom bar moved into the figure card with a hide toggle (`st.vh`); the Trigonometry unit-circle and triangle-solution figures have movable labels (`lbPost()`, offsets in `st.lab`, Reset labels buttons). Possible follow-ups: a cross-section slider for the viewer, a proofs mode in the Geometry lab, Grok art.
- **PDF Tools landing page** (2026-10-15, `mes.fm/pdf-tools`; the `tools.html` PDF Tools card now points here): static card hub from `tool_apps_src/pdf-tools-hub/` (`src="pdf-tools-hub"`; the `pdf-tools` source folder is the shared merge/split/... app), linking the editor and the six tools plus a "which tool" guide; PDF brand and info bar like the tools.
- **Read Aloud 1.7.0**: the player shows a green **Read selection** button when you highlight new text while it is open (`selCheck()` in `reader.js`; hidden when nothing is selected or the selection is the one being read). Re-run `build_site_read_aloud.py --apply` and `build_read_aloud_extension.py --apply` after editing `reader.js`.
- **Audit of the 2026-10-14/15 health calculators and PDF tools** (mobile 375px, light + dark): shared `_shared.css` unit-suffix / stat-sub colours darkened for 4.5:1, per-tool stacked-bar labels dark on amber, two unlabelled inputs labelled, duplicate in-content links removed. A small contrast / overflow / tap-target / link harness is useful for any new tool page: inject, then check text vs background for every text node, tap targets under 36px and links that 404 (a local Python server has no cleanUrls, so root links like `/tools` look broken there).

- **MES Compressor** (2026-10-15, `mes.fm/compress`, indigo `#4338ca`; card in `tools.html` "Photos & Images"; a normal page in the tools sidebar collection + bottom ad; `/compressor`, `/compress-image(s)`, `/image-compressor`, `/compress-photo|jpg|png`, `/audio-compressor`, `/compress-audio|mp3`, `/file-compressor` 308 to it): drop pictures, PDFs and audio together (paste works for pictures) and compress them in the browser. Built by `build_tool_apps.py` from `tool_apps_src/compress/` (`lib.js` = `MESCmp`, `node tool_apps_src/compress-tests.js`, 35 checks: file kinds, output formats, size fitting, `fitQuality` target-size search, PDF picture plans, MP3 plans / PCM helpers, names; `app.js`; `lib_from=["image-converter"]` for the ZIP writer). **Pictures:** canvas re-encode (halving steps when shrinking), Keep / WebP / JPEG, quality ceiling, largest side, optional "aim for at most N KB each" (binary search of quality), PNG kept as PNG only resizes (the note says choose WebP / JPEG), never keeps a result that is not smaller, **before / after drag-compare** modal. **PDFs:** Light (pdf-lib rewrite), **Balanced / Strong** (every embedded DCTDecode JPEG with DeviceRGB / DeviceGray / ICC colour is re-encoded smaller through a canvas and the stream replaced via `context.assign`; text, links and layout untouched so text stays selectable; SMask kept; Flate pictures are left alone), Extreme (pdf.js rasterises each page at 100 dpi). **Audio:** `decodeAudioData` -> optional OfflineAudioContext resample -> **lamejs 1.2.1** MP3 (64-256 kbps, mono, sample rate; MPEG-2 caps at 160 kbps). Checked in the browser: a 3000x2000 photo 974 KB -> 399 KB (target 400 KB), a PDF with a big JPEG 976 KB -> 268 KB with its text still selectable and the page still rendering, a 6 s stereo WAV 1.01 MB -> 94 KB that decodes. **Video is not supported** (the drop explains it; it would need ffmpeg.wasm (~30 MB download) or WebCodecs + a muxer: a separate tool). `/compress-pdf` stays as the PDF-specific landing page (keyword traffic) with a tip linking the Compressor; the image converter page links it too. State localStorage `mes-compressor:v1`. Placeholder logos (PIL) until Grok art lands (`compress/img/logo.png` 512, `logo-big.png` 1200x630, `img/compress-logo.png` 352).
- **MES Video Compressor** (2026-10-18, `mes.fm/compress-video`, teal `#0f766e`; `/video-compressor`, `/compress-mp4`, `/shrink-video` 308 to it; card in `tools.html` "Photos & Images", linked from a card + the Video note on `/compress`; normal-width page in the tools sidebar collection, bottom ad). Built by `build_tool_apps.py` from `tool_apps_src/compress-video/` (`lib.js` = `MESVid`, `node tool_apps_src/compress-video-tests.js`, 82 checks: size parsing "25 MB", short-side resolution fitting (even dims), rotation from the mp4 matrix, AVC / VP9 level strings, preset bitrate (bits per pixel x fps, capped at 85% of the source), target-size bitrate (`targetBitrate`, one corrective `retuneBitrate` pass when >3% over), `profile()` = weak / modest / strong from cores + `deviceMemory` -> default resolution cap, `warnings()` for free space / memory / 4K on a weak device / real-time path; `app.js`, `app.css`, `content.html`). **Engine, all in the browser, nothing uploaded (privacy page unchanged):** fast path = WebCodecs: mp4box.js 0.5.2 demux -> `VideoDecoder` -> (canvas scale when a size cap, a frame-rate cap by dropping frames, or for WebM + rotation) -> `VideoEncoder` (H.264 `avc1.6400xx` / VP9) with `AudioDecoder` -> `AudioEncoder` (AAC, else Opus) or a copy of the AAC ("Keep original audio", mp4 only), muxed by mp4-muxer 5.2.2 / webm-muxer 5.1.4 (jsDelivr, pinned, loaded on first Compress). MP4 keeps the phone rotation flag (`rotation`), WebM turns the pixels. Target size = constant-bitrate encode, then one re-encode if >3% over. "Save straight to disk" (`showSaveFilePicker` for one file, `showDirectoryPicker` for a queue, asked at the Compress click so the gesture counts; `FileSystemWritableFileStreamTarget`, fastStart false) else an in-memory blob (`fastStart: "in-memory"`). Slow path (`recorder`) = no WebCodecs encoder, the file is not mp4/mov/m4v, or the decoder rejects it: play through a canvas and `MediaRecorder` in real time (a `setTimeout` draw loop, not `requestVideoFrameCallback`, which stops in a background tab); the plan says "real time" and warns. `?engine=recorder` forces it for testing. **Gotchas found while building:** (1) phone / screen-recorder MP4s have `moov` at the END: mp4box asks for a seek, and after `onReady` the pump must `mp4.seek(0, true)` and read sequentially again (ignoring further "next position" values), dedupe the re-delivered samples by `number`, and not `releaseUsedSamples` before that (it freed the samples the restart needed: only 60 samples came through); (2) 10-bit / HDR HEVC (iPhone HLG) makes the hardware H.264 encoder fail with "Encoding error": `runFast` retries once with `prefer-software` and every frame drawn through a canvas (8-bit), and the card says colours may look flatter; (3) mp4-muxer `frameRate` must be an integer; (4) Chrome accepts Opus at 44.1 kHz, so no resampling. Tested in the Browser pane with real clips (rotated 480x848 H.264+AAC with moov first, 1080p test clip -> 720p / 15 fps / WebM VP9+Opus 29 MB -> 1.8 MB, 10-bit HEVC phone clip with moov last, the recorder path) and re-read with ffprobe / ffmpeg (decodes clean, rotation and frame counts right); the File System Access save dialog cannot be scripted, so check that by hand in Chrome / Brave. Not covered: WebM / MKV / AVI input on the fast path (they use the real-time path), multiple audio tracks, subtitles. Logos are PIL placeholders (`compress-video/img/logo.png` 512, `logo-big.png` 1200x630, `img/compress-video-logo.png` 352) until Grok art lands. Rebuild: `build_tool_apps.py --only=compress-video,compress --apply` + the usual four follow-ups (`git checkout` the churn), then `build_sitemap.py`, `build_search_index.py`, `build_aside_recs.py --apply`, `build_site_index.py --apply`, `build_redirects_index.py --apply`.
- **MES Font Preview and Finder** (2026-10-19, `mes.fm/fonts`, purple `#7c2d92`; `/font`, `/font-preview`, `/font-finder`, `/font-identifier`, `/what-font`, `/whatfont`, `/what-font-is-this`, `/font-tester`, `/font-viewer`, `/text-generator` 308 to it; card in `tools.html` "Text & Symbols"; tools sidebar collection + bottom ad). Built by `build_tool_apps.py` from `tool_apps_src/fonts/` (`lib.js` = `MESFont`, `node tool_apps_src/fonts-tests.js`, 9 checks: catalogue, CSS builder, picture-matcher maths). Type text and see it in every font the browser can really draw: a font counts as installed when canvas `measureText` of a probe string differs from the three generic fallbacks (the only way a web page can find out), so only honest samples are listed. More fonts: **Add 80 popular Google Fonts** (one `<link>` per family, loaded only on that button: the one network request, said on the page), **Add every installed font** (Local Font Access `queryLocalFonts`, Chrome), **Add a font file** (FontFace). Style: size / weight / italic / case / align / colours / background / letter spacing / line height + effects (shadow, outline `-webkit-text-stroke`, gradient text, glow) as CSS custom properties on each grid, nine "marketing looks" presets, favourites (localStorage `mes-fonts:v1`), Compare (up to four), big preview with **Download PNG** (transparent unless Background is on, 2x canvas render with the same effects), Copy as image, Copy CSS (`F.css`), Copy link (`?t=&f=`). **What font is this?** = a local picture matcher, not AI: drop / paste a picture, drag a box round one line, type the words; the box is binarised (Otsu, ink = the minority colour), cropped to the ink, averaged onto a 96x24 grid and compared (blurred cosine x an aspect-ratio penalty) with the same words drawn in every font (regular, bold, italic); top 12 with "Use this font". Tested: a Georgia bold italic screenshot ranks Georgia first. It can only match fonts the page can draw; links to WhatTheFont / Matcherator / Font Squirrel for the rest (they upload the picture). Logos are PIL placeholders until Grok art lands. Rebuild: `build_tool_apps.py --only=fonts --apply` + the usual four follow-ups (`git checkout` the churn), then the indexes.
- **Photo Editor round 2** (2026-10-19, `tool_apps_src/photo-editor/`): `PE.helpBtn(title, html)` = small "?" buttons (`PE.form` params take `help`; the option bar adds one from `OPTHELP` by label; panel headings too); every option-bar slider has a number box; **text wraps** (`wrapW` in `PE.TEXTDEF`, 0 = auto but never wider than 90% of the image, a "Box width" option and a purple drag handle on the text box's right edge in the Text tool); painting no longer inflates a small layer to the whole canvas (`beginStroke` grows it for the stroke buffer, `endStroke` crops it back with `PE.cropLayerTo` to the union of the old layer and the stroke bounding box); **Canvas size** dialog has a live preview with drag handles and Fit all visible layers / selected layer / selection (`PE.canvasRect(x, y, w, h, fill)`); **New solid colour layer** (`PE.dlgFillLayer`) and text-labelled layer buttons; a search box (Ctrl+K) over tools, menu commands and layers; **Save as GIMP (.xcf)** (`xcf.js` = `MESXcf.write`, "gimp xcf file" v0, 32-bit pointers, 8-bit RGBA, 64x64 uncompressed tiles, layers clipped to the canvas with offsets; checked against the independent Python `gimpformats` reader, and `node tool_apps_src/photo-editor-tests.js` has a structure test); **connected folder** (`files.js`: `showDirectoryPicker`, thumbnails, click = add as layer, Shift = open as image, Save this image here, handle remembered in IndexedDB `dir`); **Share as a link**: Snipboard copy passes the clipboard item as a Promise inside the click (it closed its tab when the render outlasted the click), Litterbox failure falls back to the mes.fm 1-hour host (`/api/image-temp`, JPEG <= 650 KB). Gotcha: `.pe-qpop b` must stay `> b` (inline bold inside help text).
- **Reverse Image Search round 2** (2026-10-19): the `/ri/:id` rewrite to `/api/image-temp?id=:id` was missing from `vercel.json` (every hosted-picture link was a Vercel 404: TinEye "could not read that image url", Yandex "no results"; found by running the live page in Brave, not by tests): it is there now. The link box and the picture box are both visible at once (the one used last is searched, highlighted `ri-active`; clearing the link falls back to the picture). Google Lens worked through the same flow; Bing's `imgurl:` form landed on its Images home (re-check after the rewrite is live).
- **Machine-learning teaching tools** (2026-10-19; four pages built by `build_tool_apps.py` from `tool_apps_src/<slug>/`, all in the tools sidebar collection with the bottom ad, private: nothing is uploaded; node tests `tool_apps_src/<slug>-tests.js`; PIL placeholder logos until Grok art lands; keyword redirects such as `/matrix`, `/neural-network`, `/ocr`, `/llm`, `/attention` in `vercel.json`):
  - `mes.fm/matrix-calculator` (sky blue `#0369a1`, card on `calculators.html` "Algebra & Calculus"; `lib.js` = `MESMat`, 12 checks): parse ("1 2; 3 4", brackets, `1/3`, `pi`), add / subtract / multiply (click a product cell to see its row-times-column sum) / Hadamard / scale / transpose / power, determinant (partial-pivot elimination), inverse + row-reduce + solve A·x = b with the Gauss-Jordan steps logged, rank, trace, eigenvalues (Jacobi for symmetric with eigenvectors, shifted QR with 2x2 blocks for general / complex), SVD (via AᵀA Jacobi), QR, norms, dot / cosine, and the ML operations: softmax with temperature, activations (relu, sigmoid, tanh, gelu, leaky), one dense layer `act(W·x + b)` and one attention step `softmax(Q·Kᵀ/√d)·V`. Errors name the shapes in plain English.
  - `mes.fm/neural-network-playground` (orange `#c2410c`; `lib.js` = `MESNN`, 9 checks incl. backprop vs numeric gradient for sigmoid-BCE and softmax-CE): a real MLP (tanh / relu / leaky / sigmoid / linear, Xavier / He init, SGD or Adam, mini-batches, L2) trained live on six 2D datasets with chosen input features (x, y, x², y², xy, sin 3x, sin 3y); decision picture, train / test loss curve, network diagram where every neuron is drawn as its own response picture, click a point to see the layer-by-layer arithmetic, "Try this" presets, share link (`?s=` base64 of the setup). `MESNN` is also the engine of the letter-recognition page (`lib_from`).
  - `mes.fm/letter-recognition` (violet `#6d28d9`; `lib.js` = `MESOCR` + MESNN, 5 checks): the page draws practice pictures of digits / A-Z / both in the fonts found on the visitor's computer (same canvas width probe as `/fonts`, random weight / italic / rotation / shear / scale / shift, some fonts held out as the test set), `MESOCR.normalise` crops to the ink, area-resamples to 14 x 14 and centres the centre of mass (MNIST style), and a softmax MLP (196-64-classes, Adam) trains in about 3 s with progress. Draw on the pad for live top-5 probabilities, "what the network sees" and hidden-neuron activations; **Teach it this** adds 13 jittered copies of a drawing under the right label (kept in localStorage `mes-letter-recognition:v1`) and fine-tunes; test panel = accuracy on unseen fonts, 24 random test pictures, confusion table, worst pairs. Honest about being trained on print, not handwriting (about 93% on unseen fonts, much worse on scribbles until taught).
  - `mes.fm/llm-simulator` (green `#047857`; `lib.js` = `MESLM`, 12 checks): a toy language model labelled as a toy. Tokens (words, characters, BPE merges learned from the practice text, with IDs), an n-gram model (order 1-4) with Witten-Bell interpolation, next-word probability bars with temperature / top-k / top-p, "pick next word" / "write 30 words" (seeded), perplexity on held-out text, PPMI word vectors with power-iteration SVD (8 dims) drawn as a clickable map with nearest neighbours, and one self-attention head over a typed sentence (sinusoidal positions, random untrained Wq / Wk / Wv or identity weights = similarity of word vectors, optional causal mask). Three practice texts were written for this page (no copyright); the visitor can paste their own.
- **Redshift & Blueshift Simulator** (2026-10-16, `mes.fm/redshift-simulator`, rose `#be123c`; `/redshift`, `/blueshift`, `/doppler`, `/doppler-effect`, `/gravitational-redshift`, `/redshift-calculator`, `/cosmological-redshift`, `/pound-rebka` ... 308 to it; card in `tools.html` "Simulations"; normal-width page in the tools sidebar collection, Wide default snippet, bottom ad; `build_tool_apps.py` from `tool_apps_src/redshift-simulator/`). `lib.js` = `MESRed` (pure, `node tool_apps_src/redshift-simulator-tests.js`, 64 checks): relativistic Doppler `f_obs/f_src = 1/(gamma(1 - beta cos theta))` (theta in the observer frame; 0 = toward you), z <-> beta, Schwarzschild gravitational shift kept as a log (`gravLn`, `gravZ` via `expm1`, so Pound-Rebka's 2.458e-15 keeps its digits: a plain `1/f - 1` loses 4% there), clock rate, "refractive index" n = 1/clock rate, GPS (+45.7 -7.2 = +38.5 us/day), flat LCDM `Cosmo` (comoving distance, lookback, age, recession speed; closed-form checks vs Einstein-de Sitter and the asinh age), Bruton colours, `bandOf`, spectral-line sets (H, He, Na, Ca, O, galaxy mix), real examples. Four tabs: **Near / far from a mass** (a pulse crossing a slab drawn as a refractive-index gradient: fixed coordinate frequency, wavelength = lambda0/n, packet moving at constant optical-path rate, colour of the wave = the proper wavelength local observers would measure; two light clocks ticking at 1/n; the true index differences are tiny, so "Exaggerated" scales (n-1) by `autoK` for drawing only, said on the canvas and in a note, while every readout uses the true numbers; "True scale" shows the real picture; presets Pound-Rebka, GPS, Sun, Sirius B, neutron star, black hole at 1.1 rs, falling in; far = empty = infinity; near inside the horizon is refused), **Moving source** (observer-frame wavefront rings emitted every gamma*T0, each ring segment coloured by the exact factor for its direction, three observer cards ahead / behind / far to the side (= 1/gamma), speed slider +-0.95 c or typed `0.5c` / `150000 km/s`), **Spectrum & calculator** (shift a line set by motion x gravity (rs/r) x expansion z, 1+z multiplies; emission / absorption; visible or 100 nm-3 um log scale; lines that leave the strip are listed; "z from one line" solver gives z, Doppler speed, rs/r), **Expanding universe** (z log slider + examples from Virgo to the CMB, H0 / Om editable, stretch picture, lookback, age then, comoving and light-travel distance, recession speed in c, CMB temperature). Placeholder logos (PIL) until Grok art lands (`redshift-simulator/img/logo.png` 512, `logo-big.png` 1200x630, `img/redshift-simulator-logo.png` 352). Limits are on the page (static Schwarzschild emitters, no radiation in the cosmology). Possible follow-ups: add a link from the EM simulator's Waves tab, a Kerr / orbital Doppler mix, radiation in the cosmology.

- **Site Traffic & Rank** (2026-10-22, `mes.fm/site-traffic`, teal `#0f766e`; `/website-traffic`, `/traffic-checker`, `/similarweb`, `/site-rank`, `/domain-rank`, `/alexa-rank`, `/tranco` ... 308 to it; card in `tools.html` "Media & Web"; tools sidebar collection + bottom ad). Asked for as a Similarweb-extension equivalent, but Similarweb's visit numbers come from proprietary panels, so **the page never shows or estimates visits** (said on the page; `site-traffic-tests.js` asserts `MESTraffic` has no visit-ish function). What it shows: **Tranco rank** (research list combining CrUX, Cloudflare Radar, Majestic, Umbrella, Farsight; free, no key, CORS-open, ~1 request/s so the API retries once on 429) with the last ~30 daily ranks (SVG chart with hover, best / worst / trend), domain age + registrar + hosting + DNS + mail via the existing `/api/domain-lookup` and its `MESDomain` lib (`lib_from=["domain-lookup"]`), Internet Archive first / latest capture + years with captures, and an **opt-in "Detect technology" button**. Sources rejected: Cloudflare Radar rankings (needs a token; linked out), CrUX (needs a Google API key; linked), Majestic Million (80 MB CSV; linked), so no data file is kept. `mes.fm/api/site-traffic.js` (`?d=`, `&only=wayback|tech`; tests `node tool_apps_src/site-traffic-tests.js`, 71 checks): rank is by registrable domain (small `SLD` heuristic in `registrable()`; shared platforms such as github.io / blogspot.com get a warning since the rank is the whole platform). **Tech mode is the only thing that visits the typed site**: DoH-resolves the name and refuses any non-public IPv4 / IPv6, re-checks every redirect hop (max 4, default ports only, no userinfo), 5 s, 250 KB, no cookies, returns only recognised technology names (`TECH` table: headers, meta generator, script / HTML markers) plus title / description / server header. Checked live: `localtest.me` (-> 127.0.0.1) is refused. Not rate-limited beyond the edge cache (`s-maxage` 1 h rank / tech, 24 h wayback); DNS rebinding between the check and the fetch is theoretically possible but the response carries no page content. `main_js/track.js` drops its `?d=` query. Grok art since 2026-10-22 (`site-traffic/img/logo.png`, `logo-big.png`, `img/site-traffic-logo.png`, tile `img/site-traffic-icon.jpg`). Rebuild: `build_tool_apps.py --only=site-traffic --apply` + the usual follow-ups (`git checkout` the churn). Ideas not built: CrUX / Cloudflare Radar buckets behind an optional key, Core Web Vitals via the PageSpeed API, compare two sites.
  **MES Site Rank extension** (2026-10-22, `mes.fm/site-traffic/extension/`, plain MV3, `build_site_traffic_extension.py --apply` makes the icons from `site-traffic/img/logo.png` and zips `site-traffic/mes-site-traffic.zip` (folder `mes-site-rank`); bump `manifest.json` version for a release; tests `node tool_apps_src/site-traffic-ext-tests.js`, 44 checks): toolbar popup (activeTab) looks up the current tab's *host only* (`MESST.hostFromUrl` rejects non-http, IPs, local names; paths / queries never leave the browser) through `mes.fm/api/site-traffic` and shows rank, tier, 30-day line, trend and a link to the full report. Optional **auto badge** (compact rank on the icon, colour by tier, 6 h cache in storage.local) is off by default; ticking it requests the optional `tabs` permission. Default permissions are only `storage` + `activeTab` + host `https://mes.fm/*`. Download / install section is `#extension` on the page, tile on `/browser`. Popup checked with a mocked `chrome` API (never loaded as a real extension in the dev environment). Needs the API deployed to work live. No Chrome Web Store listing yet.
- **Round 2026-10-22**: (1) **BMI chart**: `BM.chartSvg` / `chartPoint` in `tool_apps_src/bmicalculator/lib.js` draw the classic weight-for-height BMI chart (regions bounded by weight = BMI x height^2 at the scale's group edges, dashed BMI lines every 5) with your point; it sits under the gauge on all 12 calculator pages (labels reuse existing i18n keys, so no new strings), the English page links `/bmicalculator/bmi-chart?kg=&cm=`. That page (hand-made) has an interactive version above the old image (`BMI-CHART-INTERACTIVE` block; loads `bmicalculator.js` for `BM`; tap to place a point, prefilled from `mes-bmicalculator:v1`). The calculator's info bar has BMI Chart before Calculators. (2) Shear sliders: a length dragged to 0 used to rebuild the slider range as 3 x 1e-6 m and trap it; lengths now stop at fb/100 and the range never drops below fb (`lenS` in materials `app.js`). (3) Eight more composites in `MATS`. (4) **Google Ads source**: `track.js` sends `ad=g` when the URL has gclid / gbraid / wbraid / gad_source / gad_campaignid; `api/track.js` files it as `googleads` ("Google Ads" on /stats); history before 2026-10-22 stays under Google. (5) **Reminder**: Normal / Big / Huge, Fill window (W, Esc), Full screen (F) on the event card (`mes-reminder:view`); `build_upcoming_streams.py` writes `mes.fm/reminder/upcoming.json` (drives the "Next MES Stream" fill-in button) from the pre-stream pages themselves: every `mes.fm/math-qa-*/` and `livestream-*/` page carrying a `https://mes.fm/reminder?title=&t=&tz=&u=` link (as math-qa-73-thermal-expansion does) with a start not more than 2 h past. No YouTube. **Automatic:** `.githooks/pre-commit` re-runs it and stages the JSON whenever a commit touches one of those pages, so a new pre-stream page must carry that reminder link. The page itself is neutral (Google Ads target): title "Event Reminder", no MES banner or quick-start presets; the only MES item is a plain "Next MES Stream" button (no date) in the maker card that fills the form with the next stream from that JSON. (6) All Content pages (`addAllPage` sets `lpAlways`) always carry the site icons / Open in switch; the six generators that lacked link-pref wiring got it. Gotcha: a rebuild with `build_tool_apps.py` + the four follow-ups reorders the compact-nav CSS block on other tool pages (BMI, materials): pure churn, `git checkout` it.
- **Do Google Ads visitors come back?** (2026-10-22, `main_js/track.js` + `api/track.js` + `api/ads-returns.js`, a section at the bottom of `mes.fm/stats`): a browser's first Google Ads click (gclid / gbraid / wbraid / gad_source) stores `mes-adv` = `{p: landing path, d: day, n: days back, l: last day}` in localStorage (no cookie, forgotten after 180 days); a later *non-ad* visit on a new UTC day sends `adr=n`. Redis keeps cohorts keyed by the first-click day (`ads:arr|ret1|ret5|retdays:<day>`, member `site|path`, 400-day TTL; arrivals only accepted for today, returns only 1-180 days later). `/api/ads-returns?range=7d|30d|90d` sums them (4 Redis commands per day, CDN-cached 10 min). Per browser, so cross-device returns count as not returning. Privacy policy section "Page view statistics and our Google Ads" (Oct 22, 2026) and the stats page's own privacy note describe it. Tests: `node tool_apps_src/ads-returns-tests.js` (25).
- **MES Ads money page** (2026-10-23, `mes.fm/ads`, behind the `middleware.js` password like `/portfolio`; noindex, no card anywhere; a `build_tool_apps.py` page from `tool_apps_src/ads/`, ad-free, no sidebar; script + logos under `/earnings/` because ad blockers block `/ads/` paths): AdSense earnings vs Google Ads spend per day, landing page, country, device, campaign and AdSense domain, plus the "Do Google Ads visitors come back?" table (moved from `/stats`). Data: `api/earnings-ingest.js` (POST, header `X-Ingest-Key` = Vercel env `EARNINGS_INGEST_KEY`, plain project-level var; stores `adsdash:<gads|adsense>:<date>` in Upstash, 400 days) fed daily by `google-ads/ads-dashboard-gads-script.js` (Google Ads Scripts, "MES ads page", script id 12590037 in the MES.fm **manager** account: a manager has no metrics, so it `AdsManagerApp.select`s each client and counts only the accounts named in its `ACCOUNTS` list (MES (VND); MF (VND) is a client's account and MES (CAD) is old, both skipped); the key is pasted into its `INGEST_KEY` constant there) and `google-ads/ads-dashboard-adsense-script.js` (Apps Script "MES ads page - AdSense" with the AdSense advanced service, key in the script property `INGEST_KEY`, `backfill()` once then `installTrigger()`; report dates are `startDate.year` style keys). AdSense's PAGE_URL report names only part of the traffic (about 55% of page views), so totals and the daily chart come from the platform report and the gap is one "(pages AdSense did not list)" row; read by `api/earnings.js` (spend converted from VND to CAD with open.er-api, fallback 18,396.41). Both scripts run hourly and resend the last 3 days (the 6 am run resends 35), so **Today** stays current; the Ads script converts every account's costs to CAD itself (meta currency CAD). Page: ranges Today | Yesterday | 7 / 30 / 90 / 365 days (AdSense has no hourly report, so no rolling 24 h), today's bar faded + dashed spend line + an "ends at midnight Pacific in ..." note, a Totals | Per 1,000 views | Both switch (`mes-ads:mode`: earned, ad spend and net per 1,000 page views as tiles and as columns of the pages / countries / devices tables; columns carry `m: 'tot'|'pk'` and a footer `f`), a CAD | VND | USD switch (`mes-ads:cur`; `/api/earnings` returns `fx.rates` per CAD), and a draggable first column per table (`mes-ads:colw:<table id>`, same handle as /stats). Colour code: revenue blue (`.rv`, `--ad-rev`), costs red (`.cs`, `--ad-cost`), profit green / red (`.pos` / `.neg`; the earnings ÷ spend tile is green at 1.00× or more); counts neutral. Hand-written colour rules need the `#ad` prefix or the generated TOOL-DARK block wins. Tests `node tool_apps_src/earnings-tests.js`. No Google Cloud project or Ads API developer token is needed. Placeholder logos until Grok art lands (`earnings/img/logo.png`, `logo-big.png`).
